### [1] [Saddle Networks: Structure-Preserving Architectures for Convex-Concave Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28894)
*Xavier Warin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保持凸-凹几何结构的可分离分解方法，证明了混合Monge型凸性条件下的一维逼近定理，并设计了保持凸性和凹性的鞍点网络架构，在1维和5维数值实验中取得高精度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鞍点模型在优化、最优传输、鲁棒学习和控制等领域广泛存在，但现有方法难以在保持凸-凹几何结构的同时获得可处理的min-max公式和可靠的验证证书。

Method: 提出结构化可分离分解以保持凸-凹几何结构；建立混合Monge型凸性条件下的一维逼近定理；设计仅需凸性保持神经网络和简单输出变换（强制执行符号和凹性约束）的鞍点网络架构。

Result: 在1维和5维数值基准测试中，所提出的鞍点网络在光滑、非光滑和高阶凸-凹测试函数上均实现了高精度。

Conclusion: 提出的鞍点网络架构能有效保持凸-凹几何结构，并在多种类型的凸-凹函数上取得优异性能，为鞍点问题的可处理建模提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Saddle-point models arise throughout optimization, optimal transport, robust learning, and control. In many applications, the relevant function f(x,y) is convex in x and concave in y, and preserving this geometry is essential for obtaining tractable min--max formulations and reliable certificates. We introduce a structured separable decomposition that preserves the convex-concave geometry and prove a complete one-dimensional approximation theorem under a mixed Monge-type convexity condition. We then describe practical saddle network architectures that preserve convexity in x and concavity in y by construction. The proposed architectures require only convexity-preserving neural networks, together with simple output transformations enforcing sign and concavity constraints. Finally, we report numerical benchmarks in dimension 1 and 5, showing that the proposed saddle networks achieve high accuracy on smooth, nonsmooth, and high-rank convex--concave test functions.

</details>


### [2] [Manifold-based Algorithms for the Hadamard Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28980)
*Nicolas Gillis,Subhayan Saha,Stefano Sicilia,Arnaud Vandaele*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究 Hadamard 分解（HD）问题，即寻找两个低秩矩阵的 Hadamard 乘积来逼近原矩阵。提出了理论见解（包括一种有用重构）和三种新算法（基于 Manopt、块投影梯度、流形梯度下降），以及新的初始化策略，实验表明这些方法在合成和真实数据上高效且具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD 比标准低秩近似（如截断 SVD）更具表达能力，能在相同参数量下表示更高秩的矩阵（因为 Hadamard 乘积的秩通常为 r₁r₂）。但目前缺乏高效的计算方法。

Method: 1) 理论层面：给出 HD 的有用重构形式 X ≈ WHᵀ，其中 W 和 H 有 r₁r₂ 列且属于特定流形。2) 算法层面：提出三种算法——基于 Manopt 工具箱的直接优化方法、基于重构的块投影梯度法、以及无需投影到可行集的流形梯度下降法。3) 初始化策略：提出新的初始化方法提升 HD 精度。

Result: 数值实验表明新方法在合成数据和真实数据上均高效且具有竞争力，后两种算法特别适合处理大规模稀疏数据。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法和初始化策略有效，HD 是一种有潜力的低秩矩阵分解工具。

Abstract: Given a matrix $X$, and two ranks $r_1$ and $r_2$, the Hadamard decomposition (HD) looks for two low-rank matrices, $X_1$ of rank $r_1$ and $X_2$ of rank $r_2$, both of the same size as $X$, such that $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$, where $\circ$ is the Hadamard (element-wise) product. In most cases, HD is more expressive than standard low-rank approximations such as the truncated singular value decomposition (TSVD), as it can represent higher-rank matrices with the same number of parameters; this is because the rank of $X_1 \circ X_2$ is generically equal to $r_1 r_2$. In this paper, we first present some theoretical insights for HD, in particular a useful reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$ where $W$ and $H$ have $r_1 r_2$ columns and belong to certain manifolds. These allow us to develop three new algorithms for computing HD. The first one uses the representation $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$ and relies on the Manopt toolbox. The other two rely on the reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$: one is a block projected gradient method, and the other is a manifold-based gradient descent algorithm that does not require projection onto the feasible set. The last two algorithms are particularly effective for handling large sparse data. We also propose new initializations that allow us to improve the accuracy of the HD. We compare our algorithms and initialization strategies with the TSVD and with the state of the art. Numerical results show that the new methods are efficient and competitive on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [3] [An existence result for a quantitative isoperimetric inequality in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29046)
*Silvio Bove,Gisella Croce,Giovanni Pisante*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维空间中涉及Hausdorff不对称性的定量等周比$\mathcal{Q}_*$，并证明了在固定体积的凸体类$\mathcal{A}$上，$\mathcal{Q}_*$能够取得其最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究涉及Hausdorff不对称性的定量等周比是否存在最优解，这是一个几何分析中的优化问题。

Method: 通过数学分析的方法，在三维欧氏空间中，对固定体积的凸体类$\mathcal{A}$上的定量等周比$\mathcal{Q}_*$进行极小值存在性证明。

Result: 证明了$\mathcal{Q}_*$在固定体积的凸体类$\mathcal{A}$上能够取得最小值。

Conclusion: 在三维空间中，对于固定体积的凸体，涉及Hausdorff不对称性的定量等周比存在最优解（最小值）。

Abstract: We study the existence of an optimizer for a quantitative isoperimetric ratio $\mathcal{Q}_*$ in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry. We prove that $\mathcal{Q}_*$ attains its minimum over the class $\mathcal{A}$ of convex bodies of fixed volume.

</details>


### [4] [Improving depth-resolution, in-plane contrast, and reducing non-uniformity artifacts for wide-angle DBT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29133)
*Emil Y. Sidky,Adrian A. Sanchez,John Paul Phillips,Zheng Zhang,Dan Xia,Ingrid S. Reiser,Xiaochuan Pan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于宽角度数字乳腺断层合成（DBT）的两阶段稀疏正则化迭代图像重建算法，通过在第一阶段引入“背景”图像估计来吸收低频伪影，从而改善深度分辨率和面内对比度并减少非均匀性伪影。患者数据验证表明算法设计目标已实现，但还需更多任务评估来强化结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种针对宽角度DBT的图像重建算法，以改善深度分辨率和面内对比度，同时减少非均匀性伪影。

Method: 在两阶段稀疏正则化迭代重建框架下，第一阶段低分辨率重建中新增“背景”图像估计，用于吸收导致图像非均匀性的低频伪影。

Result: 在宽角度DBT系统采集的患者数据上验证，算法设计目标（改善深度分辨率、面内对比度、减少非均匀性伪影）已达成。

Conclusion: 当前患者案例表明算法有效，但需更多实证结果和任务评估来进一步确认结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Purpose: This work aims to develop an image reconstruction algorithm for wide-angle digital breast tomosynthesis (DBT) that has improved depth resolution and in-plane contrast while reducing non-uniformity artifacts. Approach: The image reconstruction algorithm is an extension of our prior work on sparsity-regularized iterative image reconstruction. The algorithm is performed in two stages as explained in a prior work. The first stage consists of a low-resolution reconstruction that exploits sparsity for quantitative accuracy. In this work, this first stage is augmented with a formulation that includes the estimation of a "background" image, which absorbs low-frequency artifacts that cause image non-uniformity. Results: The new algorithm is demonstrated on a patient case for which the data are acquired on a wide-angle DBT system. Conclusion: The results on the shown case indicate that the algorithm design goals have been met, but additional empirical results and task-based assessment are needed to strengthen this conclusion.

</details>


### [5] [Restarted Accelerated Primal-Dual Algorithms with Adaptive Stepsizes for Nonlinear Conic Constrained Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29291)
*Necdet Serhat Aybat,Jinxin Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出带重启机制的加速原始-对偶算法（rAPDB），用于求解凸非线性锥规划（特别是二次约束二次规划QCQP），通过自适应步长搜索和重启策略实现全局线性收敛，并在GPU加速下展现出优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原始-对偶方法主要针对双线性耦合问题，而QCQP等凸非线性锥规划产生的是非双线性耦合的凸-凹极小极大重构，已有方法无法直接适用，需要开发新的高效算法。

Method: 在加速原始-对偶自适应步长搜索方法基础上，开发固定频率和自适应两种重启方案，并结合单调和非单调自适应步长搜索策略；仅需一阶信息和矩阵-向量乘积；在KKT映射度量次正则性下，证明自中心平滑对偶间隙的二次增长性质并建立全局线性收敛。

Result: 建立了度量次正则性成立的条件（即使对一般非凸问题在凸多面体锥上）；数值实验表明所提方法（特别是非单调自适应步长+GPU加速）在随机QCQP和核矩阵学习实例上表现优异。

Conclusion: 提出的rAPDB算法有效解决了非双线性耦合的凸锥规划问题，具有全局线性收敛保证和良好的GPU加速实用性能，度量次正则性的相关理论结果具有独立价值。

Abstract: We propose restarted accelerated primal-dual algorithms with (non-monotone) backtracking (rAPDB) for convex nonlinear conic programs, with quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) as a special case. Unlike linear and quadratic programs, these problems give rise to convex-concave minimax reformulations with non-bilinear coupling terms; therefore, the existing primal-dual methods for bilinear couplings are not applicable. To address this challenge, we build on the accelerated primal-dual method with adaptive stepsize search -- as it adapts to the local curvature -- and develop both fixed-frequency and adaptive restart schemes, incorporating both monotone and non-monotone adaptive step-size search strategies. The resulting algorithms require only first-order information and matrix-vector products, making them suitable for large-scale and GPU-accelerated implementation. Under metric subregularity of the KKT mapping, we prove a quadratic growth property for a self-centered smoothed duality gap and establish global linear convergence of the proposed restarted methods. We also establish sufficient conditions under which the metric subregularity holds even for general nonconvex problems over convex polyhedral cones. These results are new and may be of independent interest. Numerical experiments on random QCQPs and kernel matrix learning instances show that the proposed methods, especially with non-monotone adaptive stepsizes and GPU acceleration, achieve strong practical performance.

</details>


### [6] [Global optimization of quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29294)
*Meijia Yang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆环约束下非凸二次根差最小化问题(QR)，通过建立Annulus Brickman定理将其等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)，并利用Frank-Wolfe算法全局求解。关键发现是Frank-Wolfe子问题的解（传统上视为辅助更新）被证明是原问题(QR)的O(1/√k)近似解，从而无需昂贵的二次系统求解即可恢复解。基于此无恢复特性，提出高效IMGE算法，数值实验表明其收敛快速且优于传统方法，尤其适合大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸二次根差最小化问题在椭圆环约束下缺乏有效的全局求解方法，传统方法计算成本高昂，尤其在大规模场景下难以实用。

Method: 提出Annulus Brickman定理将非凸问题等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)；采用Frank-Wolfe算法全局求解(HP)；证明Frank-Wolfe子问题解可直接作为原问题的近似解，实现无恢复求解；基于此设计IMGE算法。

Result: Frank-Wolfe子问题的解是原问题的O(1/√k)近似解；IMGE算法收敛快速，在大规模问题上显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 通过理论创新将非凸问题转化为凸问题，并利用算法副产品直接作为原问题近似解，避免了昂贵的解恢复过程，为椭圆环约束下的二次根差最小化问题提供了高效全局求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the nonconvex quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints {\rm (QR)}. We first establish the Annulus Brickman theorem and equivalently reformulate {\rm (QR)} as a 2-dimensional convex problem {\rm (HP)} with hidden variables. We employ the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to globally solve {\rm (HP)}. A key finding is that the solutions of the Frank-Wolfe subproblems, which are traditionally viewed as mere auxiliary updates, are proven to be $O(1/\sqrt{k})$-approximate solutions of the original problem {\rm (QR)}. This transforms an algorithmic by-product into the primary output and completely bypasses the need to solve the computationally expensive quadratic system required for solution recovery. Leveraging this recovery-free property, we develop the efficient Iterative Minimum Generalized Eigenpair (IMGE) algorithm for globally solving {\rm (QR)}. Numerical experiments confirm that IMGE converges rapidly and significantly outperforms conventional methods, especially for large-scale problems.

</details>


### [7] [Kernel-based potential mean-field games with unbiased random Fourier $U$-statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29371)
*Yumiharu Nakano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类势能平均场博弈问题，其中运行交互成本和终端目标成本均通过再生核最大均值差异（MMD）惩罚项表示，并开发了利用核结构的高效计算框架。通过随机傅里叶U统计量实现无偏且线性成本的估计，用神经网络参数化漂移项并随机梯度下降训练，证明了样本级几乎必然收敛定理及显式收敛速率。该方法可退化为核MMD惩罚的薛定谔桥问题，并在高维问题和电动汽车充电协调问题中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场博弈计算框架在处理核结构成本时缺乏理论保证和高效算法，尤其是MMD惩罚形式的成本函数存在计算复杂度和收敛性分析的双重挑战。作者旨在建立具有理论收敛保证且可扩展至大规模问题的计算方法。

Method: 1) 将交互成本和终端成本均建模为再生核MMD惩罚；2) 采用随机傅里叶U统计量无偏估计有限样本经验分布下的MMD，实现批量线性复杂度；3) 用神经网络参数化受控扩散的漂移项，通过随机梯度下降训练；4) 在惩罚参数、随机特征数、样本量和优化容差耦合速率条件下，推导几乎必然收敛性及显式速率。

Result: 证明了样本级几乎必然收敛定理及显式收敛速率；该方法在100维薛定谔桥问题和电动汽车充电协调问题（含个体异质性和聚合需求竞争成本）中均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一类具有理论保证的势能平均场博弈计算方法，核MMD结构允许高效计算和严格收敛分析，并能自然退化为薛定谔桥问题的求解，为高维和异质性群体博弈问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study the subclass of potential mean-field games in which the running interaction cost and the terminal target cost are both expressed through reproducing-kernel maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) penalties, and develop a computational framework that exploits this kernel structure. Both costs are estimated from finite-sample empirical distributions using a random Fourier U-statistic representation that is unbiased and has linear cost in the batch size. The drift of the controlled diffusion is parametrized by a neural network and trained via stochastic gradient descent. For this subclass we prove a sample-level almost-sure convergence theorem and an explicit almost-sure rate of convergence, under coupled rate conditions on the penalty parameter, the random-feature count, the sample size, and the optimization tolerance. The framework includes the kernel-MMD-penalty Schrödinger bridge problem as the special case of a vanishing interaction cost. Numerical experiments illustrate the method on the Schrödinger bridge problem in dimensions up to one hundred, and on an electric vehicle charging coordination problem with per-vehicle physical heterogeneity, where an aggregate-demand congestion cost represents price-feedback competition at the population level and the terminal MMD penalty shapes the state-of-charge distribution at the deadline.

</details>


### [8] [MoSSP: A Momentum-Based Single-Loop Stochastic Penalty Method for Nonconvex Constrained DC-Regularized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29635)
*Luxuan Li,Chunfeng Cui,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MoSSP算法，一种基于动量的单环随机惩罚方法，用于解决具有非凸约束和DC正则化的随机优化问题，实现了O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-3})的Oracle复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单环算法能有效求解无约束DC优化问题，但在带非凸约束的DC结构优化中保持可行性与获得良好Oracle复杂度的挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对惩罚项的Moreau包络加上凸DC部分应用单步随机近端梯度更新，同时并行计算凹DC部分的近端映射；设计两种变体：Polyak动量版本（O(ε^{-4})复杂度）和递归动量版本（O(ε^{-3})复杂度）。

Result: Polyak动量版本实现了O(ε^{-4})的Oracle复杂度，递归动量版本将复杂度改进至O(ε^{-3})，实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: MoSSP成功将单环算法扩展到带非凸约束和DC正则化的随机优化问题，在保持可行性的同时提供了可证明的复杂度保证，且递归动量版本显著提升了效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a structured class of nonconvex constrained stochastic problems with difference-of-convex (DC) regularization, where the feasible set is possibly nonconvex and the concave part of the DC regularizer is allowed to be nonsmooth. The fundamental challenge lies in maintaining feasibility for nonconvex constraints while achieving favorable oracle complexity. Although single-loop algorithms efficiently solve unconstrained DC optimization problems, their potential for constrained optimization with DC structure remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we develop MoSSP, a Momentum-based Single-loop Stochastic Penalty method for such problems with provable complexity guarantees. The key idea is to apply a single stochastic proximal-gradient step to the Moreau envelope of the penalty plus the convex DC part, with the concave part's proximal mapping computed in parallel. We derive two algorithm variants: a Polyak-momentum version with $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ oracle complexity for finding stochastic $\varepsilon$-KKT points, and an improved $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ version incorporating recursive momentum. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [9] [Solving mathematical programs with complementarity constraints by disjunctive regularizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29757)
*Sebastian Lämmel,Vladimir Shikhman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于带互补约束的数学规划问题（MPCC）的新析取正则化方法，其可行集与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化相同，但函数描述不同。该方法满足更优的约束规格，保持良好收敛性质，并在数值实验中优于Kanzow-Schwartz和Scholtes正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kanzow-Schwartz正则化方法在约束规格方面存在不足，需要一种新的正则化方法能在保留良好收敛性质的同时满足更优的约束规格，并提升数值性能。

Method: 提出析取正则化方法，利用逻辑OR运算符和等价的max型约束构建正则化模型。该方法与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化具有相同的可行集，但函数描述方式不同。通过跟踪C-指标的拓扑类型，分析了二次和双活动部分的对应关系。

Result: 析取正则化满足定制化的线性独立约束规格（LICQ），无需二阶必要条件即可保证收敛到MPCC的S-稳定点。正则化过程中C-指标的二次和双活动部分在一般情形成对应关系。数值实验表明该方法明显优于Kanzow-Schwartz正则化，在高精度求解时甚至优于Scholtes正则化。

Conclusion: 析取正则化是一种更优的MPCC正则化方法，在约束规格、收敛性和数值性能方面均具有优势，且揭示了正则化过程中C-指标拓扑结构的新现象。

Abstract: We propose a new disjunctive regularization for mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCC). Its feasible set coincides with that of the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. However, their functional descriptions differ considerably. For the disjunctive regularization, the logical operator OR and equivalent max-type constraints are used. Unlike the Kanzow-Schwartz, the disjunctive regularization satisfies the tailored linear independence constraint qualification if the original MPCC does. More than that, the favorable convergence properties - known to hold for the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization - remain valid for the disjunctive regularization as well. In particular, no second order necessary conditions are required to guarantee convergence towards S-stationary points of MPCC. Additionally, we keep track of the topological type of approximating and limiting nondegenerate C-stationary points in terms of their C-indices. Quadratic and biactive parts of the C-indices are shown to generically correspond to each other while regularizing. This is a new phenomenon as compared to the Scholtes or sign-type regularizations studied before. Numerical experiments illustrate that the proposed disjunctive regularization clearly outperforms the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. Its numerical performance is even better than that of the Scholtes regularization if solving MPCCs with high accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [Local Differential Privacy via Dynamic Quantization in Distributed Online Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29845)
*Zhiguo Zhang,Cheng Kui,Qian Ma,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式在线随机优化中的隐私泄露问题，提出了一种基于动态随机量化器的本地差分隐私算法，在保证几乎必然收敛到最优解的同时，实现了每个智能体在无限迭代下的(0,δ^i)-本地差分隐私，且适用于有向图网络，是首个在有向图上同时实现精确收敛与严格本地差分隐私的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式在线随机优化中，智能体间通过通信网络交换信息可能导致隐私泄露，现有方法难以同时保证精确收敛、严格隐私保护和有向图适用性。

Method: 设计了一种动态随机量化器，在通信前对交换信息进行掩码处理，提出本地差分隐私分布式在线随机优化算法。

Result: 算法几乎必然收敛到最优解；每个智能体在迭代次数趋于无穷时仍能达到(0,δ^i)-本地差分隐私；在有向图网络上有效；蘑菇分类、手写数字识别、脑机接口数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个在有向图上同时实现精确收敛与严格本地差分隐私的分布式在线随机优化方法，利用量化效应解决了隐私与收敛的权衡问题。

Abstract: Distributed online stochastic optimization has received extensive attention in large-scale distributed learning and other related fields due to its unique advantage in processing streaming data. However, information exchange through the communication network during the optimization process may lead to privacy leakage. To address this issue, this paper proposes a locally differentially private distributed online stochastic optimization algorithm that employs an elaborately designed dynamic stochastic quantizer to mask the exchanged information prior to communication. Theoretical analysis shows that the proposed algorithm not only converges almost surely to the optimal solution but also achieves $(0,δ^i)$-local differential privacy for each agent $i$ even when the number of iterations tends to infinity. Furthermore, the algorithm is fully distributed and applicable to scenarios where the interaction network among agents is a directed graph. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work on distributed online stochastic optimization that simultaneously achieves exact convergence and rigorous local differential privacy over a directed graph by exploiting quantization effects. Numerical experiments of distributed online training on the mushroom classification dataset, handwritten digits recognition dataset, and brain-computer interface dataset verify the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies a type of rank-based mean field game in which competing agents strategically switch among multiple effort regimes. We propose an entropy regularized auxiliary problem where the switching decisions are randomized to the control of transition probability for a continuous-time finite-state Markov chain. We first establish the existence of regularized equilibrium in this auxiliary problem. Assuming the convexity of reward scheme, we then prove that the equilibrium is unique and can be approximated by a fictitious play iteration scheme. Furthermore, as the entropy regularization vanishes, we establish the convergence analysis of the regularized equilibrium towards the relaxed equilibrium in the original MFG of optimal switching. The uniqueness of the population ranking distribution under the relaxed equilibrium is also obtained given a strictly convex reward scheme.

</details>


### [12] [A New Constraint Qualification for Continuous-Time Nonlinear Programming Based on Asymptotic KKT Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29898)
*Rodrigo B. Moreira,Moisés R. C. do Monte,Valeriano A. de Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AKKT-regularity的新型约束规范，该规范能够在无约束规范条件下，将从AKKT条件中获得的候选解集精确到经典KKT条件的水平，并证明AKKT-regularity是保证局部最优解满足KKT条件的最弱约束规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AKKT最优性条件虽然无需约束规范即可成立，但生成的候选解集不够鲁棒，有些解甚至不是驻点。因此需要一种条件来精炼候选解集，使其达到经典KKT条件的精度。

Method: 引入了一种新的约束规范——AKKT-regularity，并证明在该规范下每个局部最优解都满足KKT条件，同时证明该约束规范是保证此类性质的最弱可能条件，并给出了其适用性的充分条件。

Result: 在AKKT-regularity条件下，每个局部最优解均满足KKT条件；该约束规范是保证此性质的最弱约束规范；给出了AKKT-regularity的适用性充分条件。

Conclusion: AKKT-regularity有效地精炼了AKKT条件产生的候选解集，使其达到与经典KKT条件相同的精度，且该约束规范在保证局部最优解满足KKT条件方面是最优的（最弱的）。

Abstract: The asymptotic Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (AKKT) optimality conditions are distinguished from other approaches in the literature by virtue of their capacity to be effectively derived through numerical methods, such as the utilization of an appropriate version of the augmented Lagrange method. These tools are of a theoretical nature, yet they possess practical utility in the identification of candidate solutions to continuous-time programming problems. While this type of optimality condition is valid without imposing any constraint qualification, it is not sufficiently robust to generate good candidate solutions. In some cases, solutions satisfying AKKT conditions are not even stationary. In this study, we investigate conditions that effectively refine this set of candidate solutions with the same precision as the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. This is achieved by introducing a novel constraint qualification, designated AKKT-regularity. It has been demonstrated that, under AKKT-regularity, each local optimal solution is shown to satisfy the KKT conditions. In addition, it is demonstrated that this constraint qualification is the weakest possible to ensure such a property. Furthermore, sufficient conditions are provided for its applicability.

</details>


### [13] [Accelerating Sinkhorn for Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30267)
*Zeyi Xu,Long Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Acc-Sinkhorn，一种熵正则化最优传输的加速Sinkhorn变体，通过双层优化视角推导出Hessian驱动的Nesterov加速，保持Sinkhorn的缩放形式和每次迭代成本，在可验证稳定性条件下达到O(1/k²)收敛率，对于未正则化OT的ε-近似复杂度为Õ(n²/ε)，比Sinkhorn的Õ(n²/ε²)有显著改进，实验显示10-30倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sinkhorn算法在熵正则化最优传输中收敛较慢（Õ(n²/ε²)），需要更高效的加速方法。

Method: 从双层优化视角出发：将Sinkhorn行缩放视为内变量u的精确求解，列缩放视为v的单位步长对偶镜像下降；在此基础上引入Hessian驱动的Nesterov加速，仅使用Sinkhorn迭代的外推组合，保持缩放形式和每次迭代计算成本不变。

Result: 在可验证稳定性条件下达到O(1/k²)收敛率；未正则化OT的ε-近似复杂度为Õ(n²/ε)；在合成问题、颜色迁移和单词对齐任务上，小正则化时比Sinkhorn快10-30倍。

Conclusion: Acc-Sinkhorn通过保持Sinkhorn简单形式的同时实现显著加速，是一种实用且高效的最优传输求解方法。

Abstract: We propose Acc-Sinkhorn, a simple accelerated variant of Sinkhorn for entropy-regularized optimal transport (EOT). The method is derived from a bilevel optimization view: Sinkhorn row scaling solves the inner variable $u$ exactly and defines the reduced dual objective $f(v)=\min_u F(u,v)$, while the remaining column scaling is a unit-step dual mirror descent step in $v$. This structure yields a Hessian-driven Nesterov acceleration that keeps Sinkhorn's scaling form and per-iteration cost, using only extrapolated combinations of Sinkhorn iterates. We prove an $\mathcal{O}(1/k^2)$ rate under a verifiable stability condition. For an $\varepsilon$-approximation of unregularized OT, the resulting complexity is $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon)$, improved from $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon^2)$ for Sinkhorn. On synthetic problems, color transfer, and word alignment, Acc-Sinkhorn gives a $10\times$--$30\times$ speedup over Sinkhorn at small regularization.

</details>

Conclusion: 向量惩罚方法能有效建立约束与无约束向量优化问题中恰当有效解集间的理论桥梁，为后续研究提供新的分析框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we are dealing with constrained vector optimisation problems where the objective function acts between real linear-topological spaces. Our aim is to study the relationships between the sets of properly efficient solutions to constrained and unconstrained vector optimisation problems under certain cone convexity assumptions on the objective function using a vectorial penalisation approach.

</details>

### [15] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法虽然对不同事实进行特定权重修改，但都针对同一组关键权重子集，通过训练二进制掩码可逆转80%训练集和70%测试集上的编辑效果，揭示了编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识、导致注意力过度集中等共同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估效果，但其内部工作机制尚未被充分理解，尤其是不同事实编辑是否依赖共同机制尚不清楚。

Method: 在编辑后的MLP权重上训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码（binary mask），通过掩码逆转编辑效果，分析掩码在不同编辑间的泛化能力，并进一步分析掩码对注意力模式的影响，以及注入掩码对编辑成功率的影响。

Result: 二进制掩码能逆转80%训练集和70%测试集上的编辑；掩码通过消除后层过度注意力来逆转编辑；注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%降至38%；编辑机制是抑制而非覆盖原有知识。

Conclusion: 不同事实的编辑共享共同的功能子空间，编辑本质上是抑制而非覆盖知识，这解释了ROME和MEMIT无法传播变化到相关事实的原因，该发现可用于检测和防御不良编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [16] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过TradeArena测试平台，分析了LLM智能体在金融决策中的行为对齐与表征动态，发现失败前可测量的嵌入漂移、有效秩收缩等信号，并验证了结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号，但并非通用性能提升器。51只股票的日内实验揭示了LLM推理在相关性资产上的配置盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐机制与表征动态，特别是在市场压力下的失败前信号特征，以及风险反馈对其行为的影响。

Method: 基于TradeArena可审计交易智能体测试平台，结合风险报告、执行模拟、记忆与可重放轨迹，分析80个滚动失败锚点覆盖8条LLM轨迹，采用哈希、LSA、Transformer及白盒隐藏状态探针进行稳健性验证，并通过无思维链权重、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告等压力测试进行消融分析。

Result: 发现计划嵌入漂移、融合计划-风险表征分离、有效秩收缩等失败前信号；结构化风险反馈可改善部分模型的校准度与收益/回撤表现，但存在反馈类型与模型间的异质性；51只股票日内实验揭示LLM对耦合资产集中暴露的推理盲点。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈与表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时对齐、漂移或失败，支持研究性主张而非盈利性主张。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [17] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出微分电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）这一头部级指标，用于衡量微调过程中内部计算电路的退化程度。通过比较RL和SFT在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct适配科学问答任务上的表现，发现SFT任务适应更快但电路破坏更大、遗忘更严重，而RL保留更多基础电路但任务适应较慢，揭示了电路保存可能是RL更抗灾难性遗忘的机制原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从行为层面发现RL比SFT更能保留原有能力，但缺乏对内部计算电路层面的理解。本文旨在从机制层面探究RL和SFT在保留内部计算电路上的差异，并解释RL抗遗忘的潜在原因。

Method: 提出微分电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability），一种以注意力头为单位的度量，用于衡量微调对电路造成的退化程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上对比RL和SFT在科学问答任务上的表现，分析两者在任务适应速度、电路破坏程度和先前能力遗忘之间的权衡关系。

Result: SFT能更快适应目标任务，但会导致显著的电路破坏和先前能力遗忘；RL保留了更大比例的基础电路，但任务适应速度较慢。两者之间存在明确的机制权衡。

Conclusion: 电路保存程度可能是RL相比SFT更抗灾难性遗忘的机制性解释。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [18] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种基于轨迹感知和大语言模型推理的分子先导优化智能体，将分子优化中的工具选择建模为序列决策问题，在多项ADMET优化任务中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统先导优化依赖单步分子优化方法，无法考虑序列设计决策的长期后果，缺乏对优化轨迹的前瞻性规划。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，利用大语言模型的推理能力，将分子优化工具选择建模为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题，实现有结构约束的前瞻性分子优化。

Result: 在多项ADMET优化任务上，TRACE在优化成功率、性质改善幅度和分子有效性方面均优于基线模型，同时保持了较高的分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能有效提升先导优化效果，LLM推理智能体在药物分子优化中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [19] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大二（Big 2）这一四人非完美信息卡牌游戏中，系统比较了多种深度强化学习算法，发现PPO在受限预算下优于基于值函数的方法，且适度熵正则化和当前策略自对弈能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完美信息多智能体游戏（如Big 2）涉及隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手，现有研究缺乏在受控条件下对不同强化学习算法（策略梯度 vs 值函数逼近）的系统比较。

Method: 在统一的Big 2环境中，构建自对弈RL框架，控制输入表示、训练预算和评估协议，对比PPO、Monte Carlo Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning，并探究熵正则化和不同自对弈策略（当前策略、检查点、固定对手）的影响。

Result: PPO在随机、贪心和启发式对手场景下均优于其他算法；适度熵正则化防止策略过度确定化；当前策略自对弈在有限预算下比检查点自对弈或固定对手训练提供更强的课程学习效果。

Conclusion: Big 2可作为研究深度强化学习在非完美信息、多智能体交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下的有效受控实验平台。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [20] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的随机具身探索中，其潜在空间自发发展出与物理几何结构对应的空间语义结构。预测性能与语义对齐共同提升（斯皮尔曼r=-0.61, p=0.004），且通过KL正则化双敲除实验验证了共享驱动假设：当beta=0.1时，编码器被强制偏离几何结构，预测和语义对齐同时崩溃；降低beta至0.001则同时恢复两者。结论是物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有语言监督的情况下，世界模型从物理探索中学到什么，以及其潜在空间的组织原则是什么。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，通过方向准确性、位置表征相似性分析（RSA）评估潜在空间结构；使用20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的相关性；通过KL正则化双敲除实验验证共享驱动假设。

Result: 方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著相关（r=-0.61, p=0.004）；高beta（0.1）导致两者同时崩溃至接近随机水平，低beta（0.001）恢复两者。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的核心组织原则，这对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [21] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COM策略，通过几何约束保留时间序列词嵌入的连续性和有序性，显著提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和有序性，严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束，以保留时间序列token嵌入的连续性和有序性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致性地提升了基于token的TS-LLM性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [22] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PrismFlow，一种基于Koopman动力学的流匹配方法，通过置信度感知的胜者全得机制训练多个动态专家，在保留全局流场稳定性的同时恢复精细高频时间结构，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学，而标准流匹配使用单一全局向量场估计器，在异质时间分布中会产生过平滑近似，导致频谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法：1）引入Koopman启发的动态专家，在潜空间学习残差校正，将局部非线性时间演化近似为线性转移；2）提出置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标，仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，鼓励模式特异性专业化；3）采样时，选中的专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学校正。

Result: 在多个基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解标准FM的频谱收缩，Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数提升38.6%，在低数据设置下保持鲁棒，且对预测和插补任务有效。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过引入多个动态专家和WTA训练机制，在保持FM稳定性的同时恢复了精细时间结构，有效解决了标准流匹配在多模态时间序列生成中的过平滑问题。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [23] [TaxDistill: Improving Metagenomic Taxonomic Annotation via Distilled Genomic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28868)
*Rongye Ye,Lun Li,Zheng Luo,Yiran Zhan,Shuhui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TaxDistill框架，通过知识蒸馏技术（以500M参数的基因组基础模型GenomeOcean为教师网络）解决宏基因组分类中标签噪声问题，在CAMI2数据集上显著提升分类性能（如胃肠数据集F1从0.763提升至0.941）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于序列相似性的宏基因组分类方法受限于微生物高多样性和参考数据库不完整，而现有学习方法（如Taxometer）依赖相似性搜索工具生成标签，引入了噪声，损害了表示学习和分类性能。

Method: 提出TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架：以GenomeOcean（500M参数基因组基础模型）为教师网络提取深度语义特征并生成基于置信度的软标签，将软标签信息蒸馏至轻量级学生网络，从而减少初始检索工具引入的标签噪声。

Result: 在7个CAMI2数据集上的实验表明TaxDistill在多数场景下优于基线方法。例如在Gastrointestinal数据集上，将MMseqs2的F1分数从0.763提升至0.941，优于Taxometer基线。

Conclusion: TaxDistill为复杂宏基因组分析中的标签校正提供了一种可靠方法，有效缓解了标签噪声问题。

Abstract: Metagenomic taxonomic annotation aims to identify the microbial origins of DNA fragments in environmental samples. Traditional methods that rely on sequence similarity are often constrained by the high microbial diversity and the incompleteness of reference databases, which has motivated the development of learning approaches such as Taxometer that perform post hoc correction to learn more informative metagenomic sequence representations. However, these methods typically rely on labels derived from similarity search tools during training, which inevitably introduces noise that can impair representation learning and degrade classification performance. To address this issue, we propose TaxDistill, a knowledge distillation framework for metagenomic classification. We introduce GenomeOcean, a 500M parameter genomic foundation model, as the teacher network to extract deep semantic features and generate soft labels based on confidence. By distilling this soft label information into a lightweight student network, TaxDistill effectively reduces the label noise introduced by initial retrieval tools. Comprehensive experiments on seven diverse CAMI2 datasets demonstrate that TaxDistill outperforms existing baselines in most scenarios. For instance, on the Gastrointestinal dataset, it improves the F1 score of MMseqs2 from 0.763 to 0.941, outperforming the Taxometer baseline. Overall, TaxDistill provides a reliable method for label correction in complex metagenomic analysis.

</details>


### [24] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡多模态标签重塑（BMLR）方法，从标签侧设计解决多模态学习中因模态间映射难度差异导致的优化不平衡问题，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解模态不平衡，但往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价，且未从模态层面分析学习速度差异的根本原因。本文基于理论和经验观察，认为学习速度差异源于各模态特征空间到共享标签空间映射难度的不同。

Method: 提出BMLR（Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping），这是首个从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡的方法。通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡不同模态间的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并向各模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的广泛实验表明，BMLR能一致地提升多模态性能，并展现出与多种模型设计的良好兼容性。

Conclusion: 从标签侧而非梯度或特征侧入手解决多模态不平衡问题是有效的新思路，BMLR通过均衡映射难度实现了模态间的更好平衡与性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [25] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏置和噪声三分统计框架研究Platonic表征假说(PRH)，发现线性表征假说(LRH)通过物体-属性的线性编码解释跨模态对齐，中心化与归一化可缓解模型偏置差异，数据稀缺驱动表征噪声，并提出一个统计模型统一解释上述现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Platonic表征假说(PRH)的形成机制，即不同AI模型学到的表征为何会趋向于对齐，并试图从统计角度系统解释这一现象背后的原因。

Method: 将表征分解为信号、偏置和噪声三个统计分量。信号部分利用稀疏自编码器提取线性物体-属性特征验证线性表征假说(LRH)；偏置部分通过中心化和归一化操作缓解模型间差异；噪声部分通过分析词频与对齐度的相关性揭示数据稀缺的影响。最后综合三者提出统计模型。

Result: 1) LRH有助于解释PRH，稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐；2) 中心化和归一化能一致地改善跨模型对齐；3) 词频与对齐度之间存在强正相关，表明数据稀缺驱动表征噪声；4) 提出的统计模型能够解释多种现代AI架构中表征对齐的现象。

Conclusion: 通过信号、偏置和噪声的三分框架，本文为PRH提供了更细致的统计理解，提出的模型精炼了线性表征假说，并为解释不同AI架构中表征的对齐现象提供了统一的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [26] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen)框架，将保形风险控制(CRC)扩展到生成式AI任务（如LLM、图像生成器等），放宽理论假设并统一先前方法，为图像去重、对话系统提问充分性、AI智能体正确性等新型应用提供形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测(CP)和保形风险控制(CRC)在监督学习中提供了不确定性量化保证，但当前AI突破主要来自无监督生成模型（如LLM、图像生成器），这些模型与CP/CRC不兼容。现有方法缺乏统一框架来为生成式任务提供形式化的不确定性保证。

Method: 提出Conf-Gen框架，通过放宽CRC的理论假设，将其适配到生成式任务中。该框架统一并泛化了先前将CP应用于LLM的尝试，并扩展到了全新的应用领域。

Result: 展示了Conf-Gen的灵活性：1) 图像生成器生成非记忆化图像的保形保证；2) 对话AI系统提出足够澄清问题的保形保证；3) AI智能体输出正确性的保形保证。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen为生成式AI模型提供了形式化的不确定性量化框架，填补了保形方法在无监督生成任务中的空白，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [27] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量化基准的最小可检测效应（MDE）界，将二元配对样本量计算方法适配到量化评估中，通过预算公式帮助基准设计者预先承诺可靠性指标。在4个模型和4个基准上的实验表明，大多数NF4-FP16差异低于MDE，且交叉分割标准差接近二项式采样噪声，提示n=100子样本中的"基准不可靠性"主要是二项式采样噪声。MMLU提示模板实验显示模板变异性（2-10 pp）超过最大量化差异（3.2 pp），量化审计必须先固定提示模板。文章还提供了一个五行的预注册模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准的可重复性不足，研究者在发布量化结果时缺乏明确的方法来评估其可靠性，尤其是样本量较小（如n=100）时，观测到的性能差异可能是二项式采样噪声而非真正的量化效应。此外，提示模板的变异性可能进一步污染量化审计的噪声基底。

Method: 将经典配对二元样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准场景，推导出保守的最小可检测效应界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中m为配对项目数，ρ_d为FP16-NF4不一致率。在4个模型和4个基准上使用k=5分割、n=100进行验证，并额外进行MMLU提示模板研究以比较量化噪声与提示噪声的规模。

Result: 假设ρ_d=0.10时，所有观测到的NF4-FP16差异低于隐含MDE；大多数交叉分割标准差在二项式参考值√(p(1-p)/n)的±1.5个百分点内；OPT-WinoGrande单元在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE；MMLU提示模板范围（2-10 pp）达到或超过最大量化差异（3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: 量化基准中的大部分方差可归因于二项式采样噪声，而非真正的不一致性；提示模板的变异性是量化审计不可忽视的噪声源，必须在审计前固定；提出的MDE界和预注册模板可为基准设计者提供规划量化审计的实用工具。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBRL方法通过最小化预测损失学习世界模型，但强化学习优化器会利用模型的细微不准确性，导致模拟器被过度利用（simulator exploitation），使得策略在仿真中表现优异但在真实世界中失败，即存在虚实鸿沟（reality gap）。

Method: 将模拟器学习形式化为模型玩家与对抗策略玩家之间的零和极小化极大博弈；提出三种理论分析：在线学习保证（次线性遗憾界）、基于评论家的简化（将全局策略值差距约束为局部评论家损失）、误差-MDP对偶性（证明寻找最坏情况策略等价于标准RL问题，以一步评论家误差为奖励）。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，该方法将战略重要区域的预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍，使纯仿真训练的策略能够匹配接近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 学习模拟器的目标应为战略鲁棒性而非预测精度，通过对抗博弈框架可有效缩小虚实鸿沟，提升模型在真实世界的迁移能力。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间因果先验数据拟合网络在扩展到连续时间时面临根本性问题：若SDE在每个观测间隙只积分一次，轨迹律依赖于观测时间，先验实质上仍是离散时间马尔可夫模型。需要建立精确的连续性准则和有效的连续时间构造方法。

Method: 提出轨迹律对观测时间表不变性作为连续性准则；建立三层分类体系（离散、朴素观测网格积分、细网格积分与解耦观测）；使用随机DAG结构结合OU过程或小MLP非线性漂移实现最高层级；设计2×2（编码器×积分器）消融实验验证。

Result: 细网格积分在8/8个评估单元中优于朴素积分（符号一致性p<1/256），且评估网格越精细性能差距越大；在细积分条件下编码器轴无显著影响，但在朴素积分条件下时间感知编码器占优。

Conclusion: 提出的细网格积分与解耦观测框架有效实现了连续时间因果先验的轨迹律不变性，在药代动力学和物理系统数据上释放了先验及零样本协议。

Abstract: Extending discrete-time causal Prior-data Fitted Networks for time series to continuous time invites writing the mechanism as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) -- but if the SDE is integrated \emph{once per observation gap}, the trajectory law depends on when it is observed, and the prior remains a discrete-time Markov model in SDE clothing.
  We propose a precise continuity criterion -- trajectory-law invariance to the observation schedule -- together with a three-tier taxonomy (discrete; naive observation-grid integration; fine-grid integration with decoupled observation) and a construction realising the top tier on a random DAG with OU or small-MLP nonlinear drifts, irregular observation schedules, and hard / soft / time-varying interventions.
  A $2 \times 2$ encoder $\times$ integrator ablation, run independently on a linear and a nonlinear prior, finds fine-grid integration beats naive on 8/8 cells (sign-consistency $p < 1/256$) with the gap growing as the eval grid refines; the encoder axis is null with fine integration but time-aware-leading with naive.
  We release the prior and a preliminary zero-shot protocol on pharmacokinetic and physical-system data.

</details>

*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机决策理论在线学习中的事件级纯差分隐私问题，提出了一种无视时间范围的算法，实现了与已知下界匹配的遗憾界 $O(\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$，解决了Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定随机决策理论在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私下最优的间隙相关遗憾率，这是Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题。

Method: 算法将时间划分为指数增长的块，每个块内执行单一动作；通过指数机制并基于数据无关的随机前缀选择下一动作；利用随机前缀将块遗憾转化为所有前缀长度上softmax选择误差的和；使用单一熵势参数控制所有隐私主导的大间隙动作。

Result: 证明了对于任意时间范围T，遗憾界为 $\operatorname{Reg}_T \le 1000 \cdot (\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$，与已知下界 $O(\log K / \Delta_{\min} + \log K / \varepsilon)$ 匹配（忽略常数因子）。

Conclusion: 给出了一个无视时间范围的纯差分隐私算法，其遗憾界与已知下界匹配，解决了该开放问题；算法中的数值常数未优化。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏潜在记忆应如何存储、检索以及在非理想场景下安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器，构成模块化记忆库；2) 根据查询检索候选记忆，将查询路由至最合适的适配器；3) 使用Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享降低推理时的管理开销。

Result: 该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线方法，Self-Gating机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏建模为潜在记忆管理问题行之有效，模块化记忆库结合自适应门控机制能够更安全、更灵活地管理多个蒸馏记忆。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [32] [Do Deep Networks Forget Initialization? A Forgetting-Time View of Practical Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29152)
*Mohua Das,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Shibshankar Dey,Gareth H. McKinkey,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了随机初始化对训练后模型的影响程度，提出了"初始化记忆"概念，发现低学习率SGD能保持初始化记忆而Adam等优化器会消除它，解释了训练管线如何过滤架构先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机初始化在神经网络中诱导了一个函数先验，但实际使用的预测器是训练后产生的。作者想知道这种初始偏差有多少能在训练过程中存活下来。

Method: 提出"初始化记忆"指标——验证集选定的预测器对随机初始化尺度的依赖程度。在CIFAR-10上用ResNet进行控制实验，比较不同优化器（低学习率SGD、Adam等）和训练超参数下的初始化记忆保持情况。

Result: 低学习率SGD在达到≥99.5%训练精度时，测试精度仍因初始化尺度变化26.5个百分点；延长至5000轮后差距基本不变。Adam族方法基本消除这种依赖。更大的学习率配合显式L2范数控制也可使SGD"遗忘"初始化。

Conclusion: 训练后网络的归纳偏置并非单纯的架构先验，而是经过训练管线遗忘动力学过滤后的架构先验；那些提升泛化性能的正则化方法恰好是那些能消除初始化记忆的方法。

Abstract: Randomly initialized neural networks induce a prior over functions, but the predictor used in practice is produced only after training. We ask how much of this initial bias survives the training pipeline. To make the question measurable, we introduce initialization memory: the dependence of the validation-selected predictor on the scale of the random initialization. We perform controlled CIFAR-10 experiments on ResNets where initialization memory already sharply separates training regimes. Low-learning-rate SGD can interpolate while still remembering its initialization: on ResNet-9 with batch size $b=128$, test accuracy varies by $26.5$ percentage points across initialization scales despite $\ge99.5\%$ training accuracy. This is not undertraining: extending the same low-learning-rate regime to $5{,}000$ epochs leaves the spread essentially unchanged. In contrast, Adam-family methods largely erase the dependence. SGD can also be made to forget when larger learning rates are paired with explicit $L_2$ norm control. We interpret these findings in terms of the time scale of forgetting: gradient-flow-like dynamics can preserve initialization memory, whereas stochastic finite-step effects, explicit norm decay, and adaptive preconditioning erase it on scales governed by the size of explicit or implicit regularization. The practical inductive bias of a trained network is therefore not the architectural prior alone, but the architectural prior after being filtered by the forgetting dynamics of the training pipeline; and the same regularizers that improve generalization are precisely those that erase memory of initialization.

</details>


### [33] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）研究LoRA微调对大型语言模型内部表征几何结构的影响，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练SAE字典之间存在较弱的几何对齐，表明LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调已被广泛采用，但其对模型内部表征的具体影响尚不清晰，需要深入理解LoRA诱导的表征几何结构及其与预训练特征空间的关系。

Method: 提出delta激活框架，隔离适配器对残差流的贡献；在Gemma-2-9B上使用秩4、8、16、32的LoRA，跨多层训练适配器专用SAE；使用余弦相似度、主角度分析、CKA评估特征对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间几何对齐较弱；适配器专用SAE能更有效重建delta激活；特征密度随秩和深度增加，几何差异在各秩下保持稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可诱导出预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕获的特征结构，对机械可解释性、适配分析和微调模型安全审计具有重要意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [34] [Causal Label Recovery in Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29272)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对支付网络中欺诈检测标签的四重系统偏差（授权筛选、发行方漏报、延迟缺失、标签污染），提出序列三重稳健（STR）估计器，在理论上达到半参数效率界，在实践中可将训练延迟从数月缩短至数天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测模型使用的退单标签存在四重系统性偏差：授权拒绝导致无标签、发行方未报告则欺诈不可见、延迟导致训练时标签缺失、以及第一方滥用或发行方错误分类导致的标签污染。伴随论文[arXiv:2605.27557]已证明这些缺陷施加了极小极大下界，本文要问：这个下界能否被达到？

Method: 将观测管道形式化为具有三个倾向阶段和一个污染层的序贯缺失数据问题；构建序列三重稳健（STR）估计器，通过噪声率调整的伪标签纠正污染，用经验贝叶斯收缩稳定小发行商的逆倾向权重，提供插件方差估计量和有限样本Bernstein集中不等式。

Result: STR达到半参数效率界（渐进方差下界）；序贯三重稳健：每个门控只需倾向模型或结果回归之一正确指定；推导出最优训练延迟（成熟窗口）；STR允许训练数天前的数据而非数月前的数据；在任意样本量下STR在均方误差上严格优于朴素退单训练。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了四重偏差的效率界是可达到的，STR估计器在统计性质上最优，并将模型新鲜度与退单成熟周期解耦，大幅缩短训练延迟。

Abstract: Fraud detection models in payment networks train on chargeback labels that are systematically biased. Every label must survive three sequential gates: authorization (declined transactions generate no labels), issuer reporting (unreported fraud is invisible), and delay (pending chargebacks are missing at training time). Labels that do arrive may be corrupted by first-party misuse or issuer misclassification. A companion paper [arXiv:2605.27557] proved that these four impairments impose a minimax lower bound on detection performance. This paper asks: can that bound be achieved?
  We formalize the observation pipeline as a sequential missing-data problem with three propensity stages and a corruption layer, and construct the Sequential Triply Robust (STR) estimator. The STR corrects for all four impairments simultaneously and achieves the semiparametric efficiency bound -- no estimator can have lower asymptotic variance. It is sequentially triply robust: at each gate, consistency requires only that either the propensity model or the outcome regression is correctly specified, not both. We provide corruption correction via noise-rate-adjusted pseudo-labels, empirical Bayes shrinkage to stabilize inverse-propensity weights for small issuers, a plug-in variance estimator yielding valid confidence intervals, and a Bernstein concentration inequality for finite-sample guarantees.
  On the operational side, we derive the optimal training delay -- the maturity window that minimizes the sum of label-quality loss and model staleness -- and prove that the STR permits training on data that is days old rather than months old, decoupling model freshness from the chargeback maturity cycle. The STR provably dominates naive chargeback-based training in mean squared error for any sample size.

</details>


### [35] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构来控制扩散模型，通过条件期望算子的奇异函数学习可投影任意引导信号的低维特征，无需重训练或反向传播，在CIFAR-10上条件准确率提升37个百分点且采样速度提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中数据被噪声逐步破坏时，只有少数特征仍包含控制信息，现有方法需要重训练或反向传播，效率低下。作者希望利用生成过程的固有几何结构，找到这些关键特征以实现高效、稳定的引导。

Method: 将控制相关的关键特征表征为条件期望算子的奇异函数，通过自监督目标学习该函数基；然后将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）投影到该基上，直接注入采样轨迹中。

Result: CIFAR-10上条件准确率比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；同一表示同时支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码引导，无需辅助模型；揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，找到了最优引导时间窗口。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance是一种无需重训练或反向传播的高效扩散模型控制框架，在精度和速度上均显著优于现有无训练方法，并揭示了生成过程的相变特性。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [36] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

Method: 研究最小注意力仅变换器在全部词元破坏场景下的行为，通过两阶段经验贝叶斯框架分析：阶段1利用深度实现粒子动力学细化分布，阶段2利用长程跳跃连接作为后验推断的查询。采用固定核带宽和有限积分范围，建立了深度-噪声关系。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可解释为通过样本后验估计实现的上下文推断，无需显式密度建模，为理解变换器中的统计推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>

Abstract: A crucial component of machine learning algorithms is minimizing loss functions with less computational cost and less oscillations. While adaptive learning rate-based optimizers have been widely used for real-world tasks, they do not guarantee convergence, which is why AMSGrad was later introduced to investigate the non-convergence behaviour of Adam. In this paper, popular adaptive optimization methods like Adam and AMSGrad are critically reviewed with an emphasis on their fundamental design concepts. To address limitations of the above mentioned optimizers, a new optimizer variant, C-Adam, is proposed based on the line of sight approach. A theoretical proof for convergence is also provided and the optimizer is validated through a number of real-life based numerical experiments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 围绕四个互锁问题展开：(1) 乘积Sobolev空间框架下隐式时间步映射的适定性和局部Lipschitz估计；(2) Wasserstein-2距离的协变量偏移量化及指数级人口风险差异上界；(3) PINO训练的物理约束谱稳定性分析，证明λ_R≥λ*_R可将学习Jacobian谱半径降至ρ_F+Cλ_R^{-1/2}，实现均匀时间 rollout误差界；(4) K步TBPTT梯度分析，导出几何偏置衰减O(ρ^K)、最优窗口K*=O(log(T/σ²))及Adam收敛速率O(1/√t)+O(ρ^{K*})。在Norne油藏（46×112×22网格）上使用8块NVIDIA B200 GPU训练，30个时间步长覆盖3298天。

Result: 自回归PINO替代模型在3298天完整时间跨度上：油相R²>0.99，气相R²>0.90，压力R²≈0.80，水相R²单调提升。训练在8块NVIDIA B200 GPU上不到1小时完成。1000成员集合模拟在单块B200 GPU上不到1分钟，相比OPM有限体积模拟器获得约10⁴倍端到端加速比。所有理论预测均得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文首次为神经算子应用于三相黑油油藏模拟提供了完整理论保证（稳定性、收敛性、误差界），同时在实际规模的Norne油藏上验证了高精度和极端加速效果。理论框架中的协变量偏移上界、谱稳定性条件及TBPTT最优窗口分析具有普适性，可推广至其他时间相关PDE替代建模任务。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive mathematical and computational framework for sequential surrogate modeling of three-phase black-oil reservoir dynamics using neural operators, with particular emphasis on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) and their physics-informed variant (PINO). The application focus is the Norne benchmark reservoir, defined on a heterogeneous $46\times112\times22$ grid ($N=113,344$ cells), with a production history spanning $T=30$ timesteps covering 3298 days. Our theoretical contributions are organized around four interlocking problems: (1) functional-analytic formulation in a product-Sobolev-space setting, including well-posedness of the implicit timestep map and sharp local Lipschitz estimates; (2) covariate shift quantification, proving that the Wasserstein-2 distance grows as $W_2 \leq \varepsilon(L^n-1)/(L-1)$, with exponential population-risk discrepancy for $L>1$; (3) physics-constrained spectral stability, showing PINO training with $λ_R \geq λ^*_R$ reduces the learned Jacobian spectral radius to $ρ_F + Cλ_R^{-1/2}$, yielding uniform-in-time rollout error $|δ_n| \leq \varepsilon/(1-ρ)$; and (4) $K$-step TBPTT gradient analysis, deriving geometric bias decay $O(ρ^K)$, optimal window $K^ = O(\log(T/σ^2))$, and Adam convergence $O(1/\sqrt{t}) + O(ρ^{K^*})$. Empirical validation confirms all theoretical predictions: autoregressive PINO surrogates sustain $R^2>0.99$ (oil), $R^2>0.90$ (gas), $R^2\approx 0.80$ (pressure), and monotonically improving $R^2$ (water) across the full 3298-day horizon, trained on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs in under one hour. A 1000-member ensemble runs in under one minute on a single B200 GPU, giving a ${\sim}10^4\times$ wall-clock speedup over the OPM finite-volume simulator.

</details>

*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度学习优化中非平滑损失景观导致的梯度颤振问题，提出奇异感知Adam（S-Adam）优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性（LGI）指标动态调整步长，在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习中提升精度达6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构（如ReLU激活、量化算子）中的非平滑组件破坏了平滑损失景观假设，导致Adam等自适应优化器出现梯度颤振（Clarke次微分内冲突信号引起的剧烈振荡），造成收敛性差和泛化次优。

Method: 提出局部几何不稳定性（LGI）指标，通过随机方向导数的方差估计Clarke次微分直径；设计自适应阻尼机制exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定区域减速更新、在平滑区域保持快速收敛；采用微分包含进行收敛分析。

Result: 理论上证明S-Adam以最优O(1/√T)速率几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke稳定点；在CIFAR-100上提升精度达6%，在TinyImageNet上提升3%，有效缓解梯度振荡。

Conclusion: S-Adam通过感知局部几何不稳定性动态调整步长，为处理非平滑优化问题提供了理论保障与实用方案。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>

*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于网络拓扑Cycle-Space的检测器(CSD)，用于检测利用自编码器学习测量流形并沿Jacobian零空间生成扰动的盲目虚假数据注入攻击(FDIA)，通过最小循环基(MCB)实现最优泛化误差，且无需精确线路参数，在IEEE多母线系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于统计或数据驱动的检测方法在面对攻击者利用相同数据结构精心设计的隐蔽扰动时容易失效，特别是盲FDIA可通过自编码器学习测量流形并沿Jacobian零空间生成扰动，从而规避残差型和时间序列型异常检测器。

Method: 提出拓扑信息Cycle-Space检测器(CSD)，利用网络的Cycle-Space施加结构约束以增强零空间估计；采用最小循环基(MCB)实现最优泛化误差；不依赖精确线路参数，仅利用拓扑导出的循环约束。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57、118母线系统上的仿真结果表明，所提方法在存在实际测量噪声的情况下有效检测数据驱动的FDIA。

Conclusion: CSD通过拓扑约束而非纯数值零空间估计，改善了正常测量与受攻击测量之间的可分性，为电力系统对抗数据驱动隐蔽攻击提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven data centers and large-scale energy storage systems is increasing the reliance of power system operation on real-time measurement data and automated decision-making. However, many existing detection methods rely on statistical or data-driven analysis of measurements and can fail when attackers exploit the same data structure to craft stealthy perturbations. To illustrate this limitation, we demonstrate a blind False Data Injection Attack (FDIA) in which an Autoencoder learns the measurement manifold and generates perturbations aligned with the Jacobian null space, thereby allowing the attack to evade both residual-based baddata detectors and time-series anomaly detectors. To mitigate data-driven FDIAs which exploit the null space, we propose a topology-informed Cycle-Space Detector (CSD) that leverages the Cycle-Space of the network to impose structural constraints that enhance null space estimation. In addition, we prove that by using the Minimum Cycle Basis (MCB), the proposed CSD achieves the optimal generalization error for attack detection. By exploiting topology-derived cycle constraints rather than relying solely on numerical null space estimation, the proposed method does not require precise line parameters and improves the separation between normal and attacked measurements. Simulation results on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed method effectively detects data-driven FDIAs under realistic measurement noise.

</details>


### [41] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过随机特征回归实验发现，神经缩放定律中的指数α并非固定常数，而是依赖于优化器的选择：预条件优化器能产生更陡峭的缩放（更大的α），在自然语言特征谱附近，自然梯度法的α（0.31）比梯度下降法（0.12）高2.6倍。这提示缩放定律预测应考虑优化器因素，并提供了预测先进优化器收益的光谱诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战神经缩放定律中缩放指数α被视为固定常数的传统观点，探究优化器选择对α的影响。

Method: 在可控的随机特征回归实验（神经缩放的经典理论框架）中，对5种优化器变体和6种光谱条件进行系统测量，并分析预条件优化器对缩放指数α的影响。

Result: 预条件优化器一致地产生更大的α，α偏移在大多数光谱范围内增加，在s=1.5附近达到峰值，在s=2.0时仍然较大。在自然语言特征谱（s≈1.0）下，自然梯度法α≈0.31，梯度下降法α≈0.12，前者为后者的2.6倍。

Conclusion: 缩放定律预测应考虑优化器选择；提供了预测先进优化器何时有效的光谱诊断方法；但该指数偏移能否迁移到大语言模型训练仍是一个重要的开放问题。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文指出现代深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度的最小化归纳偏置，提出凯莱表补全作为检验这一缺失偏置的规范测试平台，并呼吁将连续平坦性先验推广到自动发现离散算法公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续领域成功但无法外推精确的算法或离散代数规则，说明其缺少面向算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置，需要建立规范的测试平台来研究这一问题。
