TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考答案、可自主驱动且具备整体推理能力的新评估体系，用于评估大模型生成的前端Web代码。该体系包含一个11领域、1000个查询的WebDev基准测试集（\dataname）和一个基于Flavell元认知监控的三阶段评估框架（\framename），在静态展示和交互式应用任务上与专家人工评分高度吻合，揭示了当前前沿LLM在交互式Web生成方面的显著提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型前端Web代码评估方法依赖于人工评判（成本高、可扩展性差）或基于参考实现、测试套件、固定检查清单的自动化代理，这些方法忽略了人类评审者在实时会话中进行的推理综合，无法高效、全面地评估交互式Web应用的质量。

Method: 提出了一个同时具备无参考、自主驱动和整体推理能力的新评估体系，包含：1）\dataname基准测试集，涵盖11个领域、54个子类、1000个查询，平衡了静态展示与交互式应用任务、三个难度等级和三组目标语言。2）\framename评估框架，基于Flavell元认知监控理论，将证据收集与判断分离为三个阶段：静态感知（被动观察形成第一印象）、智能体驱动交互（自主探索应用并捕获连续屏幕视频/音频/截图）、动态评分（在证据链完整后给出整体功能和美学判断及结构化失败归因）。

Result: 在\dataname基准上，\framename与专家人工评分高度吻合，并在13个前沿LLM的交互式Web生成任务中揭示了显著的性能提升空间。

Conclusion: 该评估体系能更准确地模拟人类评审者的推理过程，为无需人工参与的高效、可扩展的前端Web代码评估提供了有效方案，同时指出了当前最先进模型在交互式Web应用生成方面仍有较大改进余地。

Abstract: Front-end web code has become a core product surface for every frontier LLM release, yet evaluating these interactive applications at development speed remains costly because human-judged leaderboards like Arena do not scale. Existing automated proxies typically lean on reference implementations, test suites, or rigid checklists, and tend to miss the reasoned synthesis a human reviewer performs over a live session. We articulate a new evaluation regime that is simultaneously reference-free, autonomously driven, and holistically reasoned, and instantiate it through two artifacts. \textbf{\dataname} is an 11-domain, 54-leaf, 1,000-query WebDev benchmark spanning both static-presentation and interactive-application tasks, balanced across three difficulty tiers and three target-language groups, with briefs rewritten to resist recall from circulated prompts. \textbf{\framename}, grounded in Flavell's metacognitive monitoring, separates evidence accumulation from judgment across three stages: Static Perception forms a first impression from passive observation; Agent-Driven Interaction explores the application autonomously while capturing continuous screen video, audio, and per-step screenshots; Dynamic Scoring issues holistic functionality and aesthetics verdicts with structured failure attribution only after the evidence chain is complete. On \dataname, \framename aligns closely with expert human ratings while surfacing substantial headroom across 13 frontier LLMs on interactive web generation. \noindenthttps://anonymous.4open.science/r/Cookie-3CE/

</details>


### [302] [KairosAgent: Agentic Time Series Forecasting with Fused Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30002)
*Kun Feng,Ziwei Shan,Yuchen Fang,Yiyang Tan,Sihan Lu,Shuqi Gu,Lintao Ma,Xingyu Lu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KairosAgent是一个结合LLM推理器与TSFM预测器的多模态时间序列预测智能体框架，通过动态调用分析工具增强数值理解与语义推理，并采用强化学习优化，实现零样本预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，TSFM缺乏语义理解与面向未来的语义推理能力，而LLM在数值理解和精确量化预测方面表现不足，亟需一个能统一文本推理与数值预测的框架。

Method: 提出KairosAgent框架，包含LLM-based推理器和TSFM-based预测器；动态调用分析工具增强LLM的数值理解与语义推理；构建大规模高质量轨迹语料库；采用强化学习范式，包含多轮精炼和回合级信用分配。

Result: 在零样本预测任务上取得优越性能，最大化利用了预训练LLM和TSFM的效用。

Conclusion: KairosAgent为高效、可解释的时间序列智能体提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Cross-domain multimodal time series forecasting is a challenging task, requiring models to integrate precise numerical comprehension, cross-domain semantic understanding, and effective multimodal fusion. Existing approaches either build Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) from scratch or leverage pretrained Large Language Models (LLMs). However, TSFMs often overlook semantic understanding and lack the ability to perform future-oriented semantic reasoning, and LLMs struggle with numerical comprehension and accurate quantitative forecasting. To overcome these limitations, we propose KairosAgent, a novel agentic framework for multimodal time series forecasting, including an LLM-based reasoner and a TSFM-based forecaster. KairosAgent unifies textual reasoning and numerical forecasting by dynamically invoking analytical tools to enhance the numerical understanding and semantic reasoning capabilities of LLMs. The reasoning results are subsequently fused into the TSFM pipeline, enabling more accurate and reliable future predictions. To further improve the reasoning, we curate a large-scale corpus of high-quality trajectories, alongside a reinforcement learning from forecasting paradigm with multi-turn refinement and turn-level credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that KairosAgent achieves superior zero-shot forecasting performance while maximizing the utility of pretrained LLMs and TSFMs, presenting a promising direction for efficient and interpretable time series agents. The project page is at https://foundation-model-research.github.io/KairosAgent .

</details>


### [303] [From GPS Points to Travel Patterns: Flexible and Semantic Trajectory Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30014)
*Silin Zhou,Chenhao Wang,Yuntao Wen,Shuo Shang,Lisi Chen,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HTP框架，通过层级生成旅行模式再生成GPS点的方法，利用大语言模型和残差量化变分自编码器实现高质量、可变长的轨迹生成，在真实数据集上平均提升29.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹生成方法无法显式捕获出行模式，且仅能在单一条件下生成固定长度的轨迹，限制了生成数据的实用性和灵活性。

Method: 设计轨迹专用的残差量化变分自编码器（RQ-VAE），将微观GPS轨迹按粗到细的方式量化为宏观旅行模式token；扩展LLM词表对齐轨迹表示，通过监督微调使LLM能在多种条件下生成旅行模式序列，进而生成GPS点。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，HTP相比最强基线在生成质量上平均提升29.78%。

Conclusion: 层级生成旅行模式再生成GPS点的范式能有效捕获轨迹中的空间不规则性（如点密度变化），并支持多条件、可变长轨迹生成，显著优于直接生成GPS点的方法。

Abstract: Urban trajectories play a crucial role in modeling urban dynamics and supporting various smart city applications. However, privacy concerns restrict access to large-scale and high-quality trajectory datasets. Trajectory generation provides a promising alternative by synthesizing realistic data to mitigate privacy risks. However, existing methods fail to explicitly capture travel patterns and can only generate fixed-length trajectories under a single condition. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HTP}, which \textbf{H}ierarchically generates \textbf{T}ravel patterns first and then generates GPS \textbf{P}oints by using large language models (LLMs), rather than directly generating GPS points. We first design a trajectory-specific residual quantization variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) that quantizes micro-level GPS trajectories into compact, macro-level travel pattern tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner. These tokens capture rich segment spatial irregularities, such as point density variations caused by traffic conditions. Then, we extend the LLM vocabulary with travel pattern tokens to align trajectory representations with the LLM input, and apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to align the LLM with the trajectory generation task, enabling generation of travel pattern sequences under various conditions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets show that HTP outperforms the strongest baseline by an average of 29.78\% in terms of generation quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/slzhou-xy/HTP.

</details>


### [304] [RAISE: RAG Design as an Architecture Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30029)
*Zhen Chen,Yibing Liu,Weihao Xie,Yu Liang,Peilin Chen,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG架构搜索问题，并构建了RAISE框架与基准，在7个数据集上评估13种搜索算法，发现优化性能高度任务依赖，不存在通用最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统的设计选择（查询重写、分块、检索深度、重排序、上下文压缩等多环节超参数）通常依赖启发式配置，缺乏系统化评估与可复现性，亟需将这一挑战形式化为RAG架构搜索问题。

Method: 提出RAISE框架，实现13种搜索算法，在7个文本与多模态公开数据集上以标准化搜索空间和预算进行超参数优化实验，采用3个随机种子确保统计可靠性。

Result: 优化性能高度任务依赖：在某数据集上表现优异的方法在其他数据集上未必一致有效，说明聚合排名不能作为通用最优策略的证据。

Conclusion: RAISE为RAG超参数优化提供了公平、可复现、系统化的实验平台，有助于推动该方向的标准化研究。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expose numerous design choices spanning query rewriting, chunking, retrieval depth, reranking, and context compression. In practice, these choices are often configured through heuristics, hindering systematic evaluation and reproducibility across settings. We argue that this challenge is best formulated as RAG architecture search. To support controlled and reproducible study of this problem, we introduce the RAG Intelligence Search Engine (RAISE), a comprehensive framework and benchmark for RAG hyperparameter optimization, which evaluates optimization methods for RAG pipelines under standardized search spaces and budgets. RAISE implements 13 search algorithms and evaluates them across seven public text and multimodal datasets using three random seeds. Our experiments show that optimization performance is highly task-dependent: methods that perform strongly on one dataset may not generalize consistently across others, cautioning against interpreting aggregate rankings as evidence of universally superior strategies. RAISE provides a common experimental substrate for fair, reproducible, and systematic research on RAG hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [305] [Teaching Values to Machines: Simulating Human-Like Behavior in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30036)
*Asaf Yehudai,Naama Rozen,Ariel Gera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于心理学价值理论，通过超过500万道问题的实验，评估了LLM在价值诱导下与人类价值结构和价值-行为关系的一致性，发现两者高度吻合，表明价值诱导的LLM可作为模拟人类行为的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能够采用不同角色，但其是否能表现出连贯的、类似人类的价值结构尚不清楚。作者试图通过心理学价值理论来验证这一点。

Method: 使用经过验证的心理学问卷，对主流LLM进行大规模实验（超过500万道问题），评估其价值结构和价值-行为关系，并与人类数据进行对比。

Result: 价值诱导的LLM与人类在价值结构和价值-行为关系两个维度上均表现出高度一致性。加入人类价值分布后，基于价值诱导的LLM在群体模拟中的效果得到提升。

Conclusion: 价值诱导的LLM可作为有效的、具有心理学基础的模拟人类行为的工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate a remarkable capacity to adopt different personas and roles; however, it remains unclear whether they can manifest behavior that adheres to a coherent, human-like value structure. In this work, we draw on established psychological value theory to induce human-like values in LLMs and assess their alignment with patterns observed in human studies. Using validated psychological questionnaires, we conduct large-scale experiments -- over 5 million questions -- to evaluate value structures and value-behavior relationships in leading LLMs and compare them to humans. Our findings reveal strong agreement between value-prompted LLMs and humans across both dimensions. Moreover, incorporating human value distributions enhances population-level simulations with value-induced LLMs. These findings highlight the potential of value-induced LLMs as effective, psychologically grounded tools for simulating human behavior.

</details>


### [306] [Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for LLMs via Minimal Sufficient Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30039)
*Tong Ye,Hang Yu,Tengfei Ma,Xuhong Zhang,Jianguo Li,Peng Di,Peiyu Liu,Jianwei Yin,Wenhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DOMINO框架，通过归纳范式从参考样本学习领域表征，无需自然语言描述即可合成领域特定数据，在编码任务上Pass@1提升达4.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法依赖自然语言显式描述领域特征和精细提示工程，限制了在难以用语言形式化描述的真实场景中的应用；领域特定数据合成中的归纳范式问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DOMINO框架，结合提示调优（prompt tuning）与对比解耦目标（contrastive disentanglement objective），从参考样本中学习最小充分领域表征，分离领域级模式与样本级噪声，在避免过拟合的同时保留核心领域特征。

Result: 在领域定义隐式的编码基准测试中，基于DOMINO合成数据微调后的模型Pass@1准确率在强指令微调基线上提升高达4.63%。

Conclusion: 建立了领域特定数据合成的新范式，无需手动提示设计或自然语言领域规范即可实现实用且可扩展的领域适配。

Abstract: Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable progress in general-purpose capabilities and can achieve strong performance in specific domains through fine-tuning on domain-specific data. However, acquiring high-quality data for target domains remains a significant challenge. Existing data synthesis approaches follow a deductive paradigm, heavily relying on explicit domain descriptions expressed in natural language and careful prompt engineering, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where domains are difficult to describe or formally articulate. In this work, we tackle the underexplored problem of domain-specific data synthesis through an inductive paradigm, where the target domain is defined only through a set of reference examples, particularly when domain characteristics are difficult to articulate in natural language. We propose a novel framework, DOMINO, that learns a minimal sufficient domain representation from reference samples and leverages it to guide the generation of domain-aligned synthetic data. DOMINO integrates prompt tuning with a contrastive disentanglement objective to separate domain-level patterns from sample-specific noise, mitigating overfitting while preserving core domain characteristics. Theoretically, we prove that DOMINO expands the support of the synthetic data distribution, ensuring greater diversity. Empirically, on challenging coding benchmarks where domain definitions are implicit, fine-tuning on data synthesized by DOMINO improves Pass@1 accuracy by up to 4.63\% over strong, instruction-tuned backbones, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. This work establishes a new paradigm for domain-specific data synthesis, enabling practical and scalable domain adaptation without manual prompt design or natural language domain specifications.

</details>


### [307] [Learning to Choose: An Empowerment-Guided Multi-Agent System with semantic communication for Adaptive Method Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30042)
*Geremy Loachamín-Suntaxi,Robert Lazar,Dimitrios G. Giovanis,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Eleni D. Koronaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体框架，通过结合上下文赌博机、结构化智能体间通信和语义检查点，解决科学计算工作流中因语义漂移导致的策略退化问题，并在灵敏度分析和不确定性量化任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科学计算工作流不仅需要生成可执行代码，还需要选择恰当的计算策略、忠实地执行策略，并确保结果与决策之间的因果归因关系。在多智能体流水线中，智能体意图与行为之间的微小不一致会导致语义漂移，使得最终执行过程偏离原始策略，破坏下游评估与自适应能力。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，整合以下要素：(1) 上下文赌博机用于自适应决策；(2) 结构化智能体间通信；(3) 语义检查点（semantic checkpoints）以保持动作-结果的保真度；(4) 专用的大语言模型智能体；(5) 接地代码生成（grounded code generation）；(6) 自愈执行循环（self-healing execution loops）。

Result: 以灵敏度分析和不确定性量化工作流为案例，实验表明未受约束的语义漂移会损害策略学习，而所提框架在收敛性、鲁棒性和对新颖问题场景的适应性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的自主强化学习不仅需要识别高质量动作，还需要保持这些动作在智能体间传播的完整性。科学多智能体系统的设计原则是：自适应决策必须与保证语义一致性和可靠信息流的显式机制相结合。

Abstract: Automating scientific computing workflows requires more than generating executable code: autonomous systems must also select appropriate computational strategies, implement them faithfully, and ensure that the resulting outcomes remain causally attributable to the decisions that produced them. In multi-agent pipelines, this process is particularly fragile, as small inconsistencies between agent intentions and actions can lead to semantic drift, where the eventually executed procedure no longer reflects the originally selected strategy, thereby corrupting downstream evaluation and adaptation. In this work, motivated by the ATHENA framework (Toscano et al., 2025; Toscano et al., 2026) and the concept of empowerment (Yiu et al., 2025), we introduce a multi-agent framework that combines contextual bandits with structured inter-agent communication and, most importantly, semantic checkpoints that preserve action-outcome fidelity throughout the pipeline. The system integrates specialized large language model (LLM) agents, grounded code generation, and self-healing execution loops within an adaptive decision-making architecture. Interpreting the framework through the lens of empowerment, we show that reliable autonomous learning requires not only identifying high-quality actions, but also preserving the integrity of their propagation across agents. Using sensitivity analysis and uncertainty quantification workflows as representative case studies, we demonstrate that unchecked semantic drift degrades policy learning, whereas the proposed framework improves convergence, robustness, and adaptation to novel problem contexts. These results suggest a broader design principle for scientific multi-agent systems: adaptive decision-making must be coupled with explicit mechanisms that guarantee semantic consistency and reliable information flow across the computational pipeline.

</details>


### [308] [Robust and Generalizable Safety Steering for Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30049)
*Zihao Xue,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Long Ma,Zhonglong Zheng,Zeyu Yang,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Jie Xiao,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SafeDIG框架，通过位置感知的稀疏特征迁移实现扩散Transformer（DiT）的安全引导。它构建稀疏自编码器（SAE）并利用鲁棒性预训练路由选择受风险偏移影响小的干预位置，冻结SAE编码器作为可复用稀疏安全字典，仅适配解码器到目标域。推理时结合Blend（混合）和Repel（排斥）操作引导不安全激活。在FLUX.1 Dev和SD3.5 Large上有效降低了目标域和整体不安全生成率，同时保持源域安全和图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）的层次化跨模态生成机制使得安全控制与传统的提示过滤或输出检测根本不同——有害语义可能在文本表示中弱表达、逐步绑定到视觉隐空间、最终与渲染动力学纠缠。固定层的安全引导不稳定，且从已知风险学到的引导机制难以可靠迁移到目标风险域。

Method: 1) 在功能不同的DiT干预位置上构建稀疏自编码器（SAE）；2) 使用鲁棒性预训练路由，优先选择在源-目标风险偏移下保持稳定的干预位点；3) 冻结SAE编码器作为可复用稀疏安全字典，仅适配解码器到目标域激活流形；4) 推理时采用Blend（将不安全激活向安全流形混合）和Repel（将激活推离有害稀疏方向）操作进行安全引导。

Result: 在FLUX.1 Dev和Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large上进行实验，SafeDIG一致降低了目标域和整体的不安全生成率，同时保持了源域的安全性和图像质量。

Conclusion: SafeDIG通过位置感知的稀疏特征迁移有效解决了DiT安全引导中的跨域迁移不稳定问题，验证了将安全特征与域特定激活几何分离、冻结稀疏安全字典并仅适配解码器的有效性，为扩散模型的安全控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become a powerful backbone for text-to-image generation, but their layered and cross-modal generation process makes safety control fundamentally different from prompt-level filtering or output-level detection. Harmful semantics may be weakly expressed in text representations, progressively bound to visual latents, and finally entangled with rendering dynamics. As a result, safety steering at a fixed layer can be unstable, and a steering mechanism learned from known risks may not transfer reliably to a shifted target risk domain. We propose SafeDIG, a safety steering framework that formulates DiT safety adaptation as position-aware sparse feature transfer. SafeDIG first constructs Sparse Autoencoders over functionally distinct DiT intervention positions and uses robustness-aware pre-training routing to prioritize intervention sites that are expected to remain stable under source-target risk shift. It then separates transferable safety features from domain-specific activation geometry by freezing the SAE encoder as a reusable sparse safety dictionary and adapting only the decoder to the target-domain activation manifold. During inference, SafeDIG combines Blend and Repel operations to steer unsafe activations toward transferred safety manifolds or away from harmful sparse directions. Experiments on FLUX.1 Dev and Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large show that SafeDIG consistently reduces target-domain and overall unsafe generation rates while preserving source-domain safety and image quality.

</details>


### [309] [Selective QA over Conflicting Multi-Source Personal Memory: A Diagnostic Testbed and Method Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30087)
*Tiancheng Yang,Matthias Schonlau,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向个人AI代理的冲突多源记忆选择性问答问题，构建了包含34,560个实例的基准测试集，评估了多种方法，发现最佳融合解析器达到80.3%准确率，引入弃权机制后选择性准确率达85.3%（覆盖率78.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以区分错误源于证据本身还是方法的冲突解决步骤，亟需一种能精准评估系统在冲突、不完整证据下推理能力的评测方法。

Method: 构建了包含18个问题模板、8种推理类型、480个角色、4个随机种子的34,560实例基准测试，通过受控源扭曲和确定性真值，评估无源、单源、结构化融合方法及前沿LLM的性能。

Result: 最佳训练融合解析器准确率80.3%，最强纯提示LLM基线70.0%；引入弃权后融合解析器选择性准确率85.3%（覆盖率78.3%），最佳LLM为71.0%（覆盖率95.4%）。不同模型在不同推理类型上表现各异。

Conclusion: 当前方法在冲突多源记忆问答上仍有较大提升空间，融合方法优于纯LLM提示，不同推理类型需针对性优化，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Emerging personal AI agents are moving toward persistent, multi-source memory. This creates an evaluation problem: systems must decide how to use conflicting or incomplete evidence; they cannot just retrieve facts from one clean history. Existing benchmarks rarely show whether an error came from the evidence given to a method or from the method's conflict-resolution step. We study this as selective QA over conflicting multi-source personal memory: systems answer based on conflicting, sometimes incomplete sources, or abstain when evidence is insufficient. We develop a benchmark containing 18 question templates across 8 reasoning types, 480 personas, 4 random seeds, and 34,560 instances, with controlled source distortions and deterministic ground truth. We evaluate the performance of baselines without access to any source, access to a single source, structured fusion methods, and frontier LLMs. The best trained fusion resolver reaches 80.3% accuracy, while the strongest prompt-only LLM baseline reaches 70.0%. With abstention, the same resolver reaches 85.3% selective accuracy at 78.3% coverage and the best LLM reaches 71.0% selective accuracy at 95.4% coverage. Different models have different strengths across reasoning types. We release the data, code, cached model outputs, and data-generating process for reuse.

</details>


### [310] [PokerSkill: LLMs Can Play Expert-Level Poker without Training or Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30094)
*Boning Li,Baoxiang Wang,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PokerSkill 是一个无需训练和求解器的框架，通过将详细的规则型扑克技能作为结构化动作接口与LLM结合，使LLM在复杂不完全信息游戏（德州扑克）中达到与基于求解器的系统竞争的性能，相比默认提示基线减少49-61%的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑克是人工智能的重大挑战。现有方法依赖基于反事实遗憾最小化的均衡求解器，需要数百万核心小时的训练；LLM虽拥有大量扑克知识但直接游戏表现远逊于求解器智能体；传统规则型智能体可解释且无需训练，但策略上限远低于均衡玩法。作者希望弥合这一差距，提出一种既无需训练也无需求解器的方法。

Method: 提出PokerSkill框架，包含：(1) 由人类扑克专家设计的层级化技能库，包含详细的规则型扑克技能；(2) 确定性上下文引擎，分析当前牌局状态并从技能库中检索相关片段；(3) 将检索到的规则技能作为结构化动作接地接口约束LLM选择合理动作。整个流程无需训练和求解器。

Result: 在GTOWizard基准上，GPT-5.5 XHigh + PokerSkill达到 -57±21 mbb/hand，Claude Opus 4.6达到 -80±29 mbb/hand，Claude Opus 4.7达到 -87±64 mbb/hand，相比默认提示基线减少49-61%的损失，并优于强智能体Slumbot。

Conclusion: 规则型技能本身不足以构成强策略，LLM本身也无法玩好，但二者结合产生了一个无需训练和求解器却能与基于数百万核心小时计算构建的系统竞争的智能体。这是首次证明LLM在复杂不完全信息游戏中无需特定游戏训练或求解器查询即可达到竞争性表现。

Abstract: Poker is a landmark challenge for artificial intelligence. The dominant approach relies on equilibrium solvers built on counterfactual regret minimization, requiring millions of core-hours of training. Large Language Models (LLMs) possess extensive poker knowledge but perform far below solver-based agents when asked to play directly. Traditional rule-based poker agents are interpretable and training-free, but their strategic ceiling remains far below equilibrium play. We introduce \textbf{PokerSkill}, a training-free and solver-free framework that bridges this gap by using detailed rule-based poker skills as a structured action-grounding interface for LLMs. A deterministic context engine analyzes the current state and retrieves only the relevant fragments from a layered skill library, which is entirely designed by human poker experts, constraining the LLM's choice to reasonable actions. Against GTOWizard, a state-of-the-art GTO benchmark, GPT-5.5 XHigh with PokerSkill achieves $-57 \pm 21$ mbb/hand, Claude Opus 4.6 achieves $-80 \pm 29$ mbb/hand and Claude Opus 4.7 achieves $-87\pm 64$ mbb/hand, reducing losses by 49--61\% compared to default-prompt baselines and outperforming the strong bot Slumbot. Our key finding is that rule-based skills alone do not constitute a strong strategy, and LLMs alone cannot play well, but their combination yields an agent that requires neither training nor solver access yet competes with systems built on millions of core-hours of computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an LLM achieving competitive performance in a complex imperfect-information game without game-specific training or solver queries. Code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/PokerSkill.

</details>


### [311] [VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117)
*Haoyuan Shi,Xiancong Ren,Yingji Zhang,Qinfan Zhang,Jiayu Hu,Haozhe Shan,Han Dong,Jinpeng Lu,Yinda Chen,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLA-Trace是一个渐进式诊断框架，通过表示动力学、因果控制归因和行为表现三个层次的统一证据链，分析了π₀.₅和OpenVLA两种VLA模型，发现它们具有不同的模态适应动力学、多模态路由策略，且在细粒度语义跟随方面存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLA模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制仍是一个未解决的挑战，现有方法缺乏系统性的诊断框架来追踪从表示到行为的完整证据链。

Method: 提出了VLA-Trace框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 结合跨模态和以检查点漂移为中心的CKA来追踪表示演化；2) 注意力消融干预来识别模态特定控制通路；3) rollout级行为探测来检验 grounding、捷径依赖和语义跟随能力。

Result: 1) 两种模型在VLA微调中表现出不同的模态适配动力学；2) 它们在动作解码时依赖不同的多模态路由策略和层级依赖；3) VLA策略在视觉引导的轨迹生成上表现优异，但在细粒度语义跟随上存在局限。

Conclusion: 这些发现指出了未来方向：保持表示的适配方法、因果VLA电路研究以及组合语义控制。

Abstract: Understanding how Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform multimodal knowledge into embodied control remains an open challenge. We present VLA-Trace, a progressive diagnostic framework that analyzes VLA models through a unified evidence chain from representation dynamics to causal control attribution and behavioral manifestation. It specifically combines cross-modal and checkpoint-drift centered kernel alignment (CKA) to trace representation evolution, attention knockout interventions to identify modality-specific control pathways, and rollout-level behavioral probes to examine grounding, shortcut dependence, and semantic following. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA reveal three key findings. First, the two models exhibit distinct modality-specific adaptation dynamics during VLA finetuning. Second, they rely on different multimodal routing strategies and layer-wise dependencies during action decoding. Third, although VLA policies excel at visually grounded trajectory generation, they remain limited in fine-grained semantic following. These findings highlight future directions for representation-preserving adaptation, causal VLA circuits, and compositional semantic control.

</details>


### [312] [Enhancing Multi-Agent Communication through Attention Steering with Context Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30136)
*Hongxiang Zhang,Yuan Tian,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent-Radar是一种无需训练的上下文管理方法，通过新颖的时间与空间衰减机制动态引导多智能体系统关注相关上下文，有效解决长对话历史导致的信息稀释问题，在五个基准上取得最高7.64个绝对点的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在交互过程中会迅速积累极长的对话历史，相关信息被无关上下文稀释，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出Agent-Radar，一种免训练的上下文管理方法，通过时间与空间衰减机制动态引导每个智能体关注相关上下文。

Result: 在五个不同基准上超越现有方法，最高提升7.64个绝对点；随着智能体数量和交互轮次增加仍保持有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Agent-Radar的核心组件对性能至关重要，且在不同设置下具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex tasks through collaborative reasoning. However, these systems tend to rapidly accumulate extremely long conversation histories during interaction. As conversations lengthen, relevant information is increasingly diluted by irrelevant context, leading to degraded performance. In this work, we present Agent-Radar, a training-free context management method that dynamically steers each agent's attention toward relevant context with a novel temporal and spatial decay mechanism. Our experiments demonstrate that Agent-Radar outperforms state-of-the-art methods across five different benchmarks, yielding gains of up to 7.64 absolute points. Furthermore, our analysis shows that Agent-Radar remains effective and robust as the number of agents and interaction rounds increases. Finally, the ablation study shows that core components in Agent-Radar are crucial to performance and generalizable in different settings.

</details>


### [313] [AgentSchool: An LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30144)
*Yulei Ye,Wenhao Li,Zhong Wen,Yunshu Huang,Yichen Hu,Zifan Wei,Yige Wang,Xinyu Xie,Haoxuan Yang,Yanjun Huang,Ruijia Li,Hong Qian,Yu Song,Bo Jiang,Bingdong Li,Lijun Li,Bo Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Xingcheng Xu,Shuangye Chen,Xia Hu,Liang He,Aimin Zhou,Jingjing Qu,Jing Shao,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSchool是一个基于LLM的多智能体教育模拟器，通过可成长的学生智能体（知识图谱、思维工作流、错误概念）和自适应教师智能体（基于最近发展区规划与反思），在可配置的场景生成器和多尺度模拟器支持下，实现了比基线更精细的学习轨迹模拟，并产生了符合课堂社会理论的行为模式，同时将教育定位为长期记忆、多智能体协调和组织压力下推理的社会性测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM部署到课堂中时，验证教育AI面临独特困难：干预作用于发展中的学习者（其认知和社会轨迹不可逆），而真实实验周期长、受伦理约束且受制度限制。现有LLM教育模拟器大多将学习降维为人设驱动的角色扮演，且若仅优化以复现现有课堂，会结构性惩罚教学改革所需的制度创新。

Method: 构建多智能体模拟器AgentSchool，包含：(1) 可成长的学生智能体——加权学科知识图谱、思维工作流池、显式错误概念；(2) 自适应教师智能体——基于最近发展区（ZPD）进行规划、搭建支架和反思；(3) 可配置场景生成器——涵盖正式和非正式学习场景；(4) 多尺度模拟器——解耦交互规模、时间粒度和模拟时长。

Result: 结构化学生智能体相比基线模拟器产生了更差异化的掌握度和错误概念轨迹；教师智能体对比显示出与ZPD适应一致的主干网络依赖模式；AgentSchool生成了符合课堂社会理论的边缘参与、小团体形成、攻击者引发的凝聚力以及意见领袖涌现等可信轨迹。

Conclusion: AgentSchool不仅可作为教育研究工具，更将教育定位为长期记忆、多智能体协调以及组织压力下未来制度推理的社会性有意义的测试平台。

Abstract: Despite the rapid deployment of LLMs into classrooms, validating educational AI remains uniquely intractable: interventions act on developing learners whose cognitive and social trajectories are irreversibly shaped, while real-world trials are slow, ethically constrained, and institutionally locked. LLM-based educational simulators have emerged as a potential remedy, but many still collapse learning into persona-conditioned role-play and, when optimized only to reproduce existing classrooms, can structurally penalize the institutional novelty that pedagogical reform requires. In this work, we introduce AgentSchool, an LLM-driven multi-agent simulator that models learning as state transition rather than prompted behavior. AgentSchool couples cognitively growable student agents -- equipped with weighted subject knowledge graphs, thinking-workflow pools, and explicit misconceptions -- with adaptive teacher agents that plan, scaffold, and reflect along the Zone of Proximal Development, embedded in a configurable scenery generator that situates instruction within both formal and informal learning fields, and a multi-scale simulator that decouples interaction scale, temporal granularity, and simulation duration. Experiments show that structured student agents produce more differentiated mastery and misconception traces than a baseline simulator, while teacher-agent comparisons show backbone-dependent patterns consistent with ZPD-informed adaptation. Further, AgentSchool generates plausible traces of peripheral participation, clique formation, aggressor-induced cohesion, and opinion-leader emergence consistent with classroom social theories. Beyond its role as an educational research instrument, AgentSchool frames education as a socially meaningful testbed for long-horizon memory, multi-agent coordination, and future institutional reasoning under organizational pressure.

</details>


### [314] [Anchorless Diversification for Parallel LLM Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30150)
*Fares Nabil Ibrahim,Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究LLM在创意任务中通过推理时控制实现候选池多样化的方法，发现无锚点方法（特别是语义方向分层）在多样性-质量-计算效率方面优于依赖种子想法的有锚点方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于生成创意候选池，但如何在不依赖已有种子想法的情况下高效实现多样化探索尚不清楚。需要建立实用且成本效益高的无锚点多样化基线方法。

Method: 在三种创意任务家族中，比较了独立生成、语义方向分层与自锚点、同伴锚点、代表锚点等基线方法，在中性指令和群体参照发散指令下进行评估。采用全流水线token核算方法进行公平比较。

Result: 群体参照发散指令是低成本强基线，能在保持质量代理指标的同时增加语义多样性。语义方向分层最优，仅需一次规划调用即可组织跨方向生成，实现最佳多样性-质量-计算前沿。锚点再生在最终池多样性上有优势，但全流水线核算下优势减弱。

Conclusion: 建立了面向开放式LLM创意生成任务的实用无锚点多样化基线，表明无锚点方法可以媲美甚至超越依赖种子想法的方法。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to generate candidate-idea pools for creative tasks where broad exploration is valuable. Parallel inference can be attractive in this setting when it broadens the pool while retaining quality and cost efficiency. We study inference-time controls for candidate-pool diversification, asking whether anchorless methods can rival methods that depend on observed seed ideas. Across three creative task families, we compare independent generation and semantic direction stratification with self-, peer-, and representative-anchor baselines, under neutral and population-referential divergent instructions. Population-referential divergence is a strong low-cost baseline, increasing semantic diversity while preserving quality proxies. Semantic direction stratification is stronger: a single planning call organizes generations across broad semantic directions, yielding the best diversity--quality--compute frontier. Anchored regeneration can be strong in final-pool diversity, but its advantage shrinks under full-pipeline token accounting. These results establish practical anchorless baselines for open-ended LLM ideation.

</details>


### [315] [Temporal Stability and Few-Shot Prompting in Math Task Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30151)
*Danielle S. Fox,Brenda L. Robles,Elizabeth DiPietro Brovey,Christian D. Schunn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究纵向考察了AI工具（Gemini和Coteach）使用任务分析指南（TAG）对数学任务认知需求进行分类的能力，发现模型版本更新效果不一（Gemini稳定在58%，Coteach从75%降至50%），而少样本提示显著提升两者性能（Gemini达67%，Coteach恢复至75%），表明提示工程技术比被动模型更新更可靠有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在教育场景中的集成日益深入，需要了解其在专业教育任务上的稳定性以及对提示工程技术的响应性，以指导教育者和研究者进行AI工具的选择、评估和实施。

Method: 采用纵向研究设计，测试通用AI工具（Gemini）和教育专用AI工具（Coteach）使用TAG分类数学任务认知需求的能力。在基线测试、模型版本更新后复测、以及使用少样本提示（每个认知需求类别提供两个示例任务）后再次测试，比较三种条件下的分类准确率。

Result: Gemini的准确率在版本更新后稳定在58%，Coteach则从75%下降至50%。少样本提示后，Gemini提升至67%，Coteach恢复至75%准确率。

Conclusion: 提示工程技术对AI工具在专业教育任务上的表现具有比被动模型更新更大且更可靠的影响，版本更新并不总是带来性能提升，这对教育场景中AI工具的选择、评估和实施具有重要启示。

Abstract: As AI tools become increasingly integrated into educational contexts, questions arise about both their stability over time and their responsiveness to prompt engineering techniques. This longitudinal study focused on different AI tools' ability to use the Task Analysis Guide (TAG; Stein \& Smith, 1998) to classify the cognitive demand of mathematics tasks. In particular, it examined whether this classification ability changed with (1) model version updates over time and (2) few-shot prompting using exemplar tasks. We tested a general-purpose AI tool (Gemini) and an education-specific AI tool (Coteach). The specific tools were selected because of their relatively high performance on relevant published benchmarks and prior task-specific tests. Models were tested at baseline, retested with model version updates, and then tested again using few-shot prompting (two exemplar tasks for each cognitive demand category). Results revealed that newer model versions alone produced mixed effects: Gemini's accuracy remained stable at 58\%, while Coteach's accuracy decreased from 75\% to 50\%. However, few-shot prompting improved both models' performance: Gemini increased to 67\% and Coteach recovered to 75\% accuracy. These findings demonstrate that prompt engineering techniques can have larger and more reliable effects than passive model improvements, and that version updates may not always improve performance on specialized educational tasks. The study has important implications for how educators and researchers should approach AI tool selection, evaluation, and implementation in educational contexts.

</details>


### [316] [Meta-Cognitive Memory Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30159)
*Ziyan Liu,Zhezheng Hao,Yeqiu Chen,Hong Wang,Jingren Hou,Ruiyi Ding,Yongkang Yang,Wence Ji,Wei Xia,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出元认知记忆策略优化（MMPO），通过信念熵作为自监督代理指标，对导致高认知不确定性的中间记忆摘要进行细粒度惩罚，从而优化长时域任务中LLM代理的记忆质量，在175万token上下文中仍保持97.1%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆增强的LLM代理通常使用结果导向的强化学习训练记忆策略，无法定位中间记忆质量的退化点。递归摘要逐渐丢弃任务相关信息并引入语义噪声，加剧信念偏差，影响代理对潜在任务状态的估计，最终导致长时域推理失败。

Method: 提出信念熵（Belief Entropy）作为自监督代理指标，量化模型在当前记忆下对潜在任务状态的不确定性；基于此设计元认知记忆策略优化（MMPO），在稀疏结果信号之外，通过显式惩罚引发高认知不确定性的摘要来提供细粒度的记忆级监督。

Result: MMPO在多种长时域任务中一致优于现有方法，在扩展到175万token上下文时仍保持97.1%的性能。

Conclusion: 记忆优化不应仅关注轨迹级成功，而应关注中间摘要所诱导的信念清晰度；基于信念熵的细粒度监督能有效提升长时域推理的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents tackle complex long-horizon tasks by recursively summarizing interaction trajectories into compact memory. However, existing approaches typically train these memory policies using outcome-based reinforcement learning, failing to localize where intermediate memory quality degrades. As interactions unfold, ambiguous recursive summaries progressively discard task-relevant information and introduce semantic noise. This exacerbates belief deviation, obscuring the agent's estimate of the latent task state and ultimately derailing long-horizon reasoning. We therefore argue that memory optimization should focus not merely on trajectory-level success, but on the clarity of the belief induced by intermediate summaries. To this end, we introduce Belief Entropy, a self-supervised proxy that probes how uncertain the model remains about the latent task state given its current memory. Based on this proxy, we propose Metacognitive Memory Policy Optimization (MMPO). Instead of relying only on sparse outcome-based signals, MMPO provides fine-grained, memory-specific supervision via explicitly penalizing summaries that induce high epistemic uncertainty. Experiments show that MMPO consistently outperforms existing methods on diverse long-horizon tasks, maintaining 97.1% performance even when scaled to 1.75M-token contexts.

</details>


### [317] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对语言模型的生物安全评估进行了补充性研究，不仅关注模型是否拒绝生成有害内容，更考察拒绝机制的结构稳健性。实验发现，在五种架构中，没有模型能清晰区分良性查询与危险查询：Gemma 2 2B-IT从未真正拒绝任何提示；Gemma 4 E2B-IT在有对话模板时拒绝65/75提示，无模板时为0/75；两个Gemma模型在80词上限下完全崩溃；Qwen和Phi-3过度拒绝良性疾病问题（83-87%）；仅Llama 3.2 1B呈现有意义的分级行为。论文进一步通过合法但非毒性化合物（如迷幻蘑菇培养）测试，发现拒绝机制更多追踪法律和文化显著性而非实际生物危害。内部一致性方面，提出散度分数D，比较模型表面响应标签与稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征激活，在Gemma 4上发现遵从事和拒绝事的激活差异为0.647分且无重叠（n=75），但初步证据仅限于一个周末的消费者硬件实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物安全评估仅关注模型是否输出有害内容，忽略了拒绝机制的结构稳健性。本文提出互补性问题：当模型拒绝时，这种拒绝在提示框架、格式或输出长度的微小变化下是否仍然存在？同时探索如何利用内部激活级审计（通过SAE）揭示行为评估无法发现的失效模式。

Method: 1) 行为评估：对比五种架构（Gemma 2 2B-IT、Gemma 4 E2B-IT、Qwen 2.5 1.5B、Phi-3-mini、Llama 3.2 1B）在75个提示上的拒绝行为，变化提示框架、格式和输出长度。2) 过度拒绝探测：使用合法但无毒性的Schedule I化合物（如迷幻蘑菇培养）测试模型拒绝是否追踪法律显著性而非实际生物危害。3) 内部审计：提出散度分数D，对比模型表面响应标签与SAE特征激活的一致性。

Result: 1) 无模型能干净区分良性/危险查询；Gemma 2 2B-IT从未真正拒绝；Gemma 4 E2B-IT的拒绝完全依赖对话模板；两个Gemma在80词上限下拒绝率为0%。2) Qwen和Phi-3过度拒绝（83-87%良性疾病标记为危险）。3) 仅Llama 3.2 1B呈现有意义的梯度（61分跨度）。4) 拒绝更多追踪法律/文化显著性而非CBRN危害。5) Gemma 4上，遵从事和拒绝事的SAE激活差异0.647分且无重叠（n=75）。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，激活级审计（通过SAE）可以揭示行为评估不可见的失效模式，且不同架构间存在显著差异。但当前结果受限于窄目录、样本内校准、仅Gemma系列SAE覆盖，以及消费者硬件上的单一周末实验。

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [318] [Modularizing Educational LLM-Agency for Fostering Responsible Learning Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30187)
*Julius Gabelmann,Felix Jahn,Kevin Baum,Sophie van Rossum,Emely Wuenscher,Timo P. Gros,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体架构的AI聊天机器人，通过模块化设计将教学法建议融入解题辅导过程，旨在实现更负责任、可控、透明的人工智能教育应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人在教育中的广泛采用可能对学习过程产生负面影响（如迁移能力、批判性思维和创造力的丧失），而现有的大语言模型虽然能获取教育科学知识，但缺乏遵循教学法概念的倾向，因此需要设计专门面向负责任AI教育应用的架构。

Method: 首先识别负责任的LLM教育系统应满足的若干期望标准，论证单体式现成解决方案的结构性缺陷，提出将智能体架构模块化，针对解题的不同阶段设计特定模块，从而融入有针对性的教学法建议，实现更可控、透明和可监督的学习引导过程。

Result: 提出了一种模块化的智能体AI聊天机器人架构，能够将教学法建议分阶段融入学生解题辅导过程中。

Conclusion: 模块化的智能体架构相较于单体式方案更能满足负责任AI教育系统的需求，通过可控、透明的方式引导学生学习过程，有助于降低AI在教育中应用的负面影响。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of AI chatbots in education will drastically change learning, making responsible deployment a critical concern. While large language models (LLMs) might have access to sources discussing insights from educational sciences, they are not particularly inclined to adhere to pedagogical concepts, risking negative effects on the learning process, such as a loss of transfer capabilities, critical thinking, or creativity. In this paper, we introduce an agentic AI chatbot architecture assisting students with exercise solving, specifically designed to contribute to more responsible AI use in education. We base our conceptual development on the identification of several desiderata for responsible LLM-based educational systems, argue for the structural shortcomings inherent in monolithic, out-of-the-box solutions, and instead suggest modularizing the agentic architecture. We propose specific modules for different stages of exercise solving, enabling incorporation of targeted pedagogical advice, guiding students through the learning process in a more controllable, transparent, and overseeable manner.

</details>


### [319] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang,Yuwen Yang,Zhen Wang,Ming Ma,Ding Yu,Chentai Wang,Keman Huang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了K-12教育中LLM、教师和学生三方协作系统，通过包含57,954篇作文的大规模实证数据集，验证了LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师负担、教师作为教学守门人保证反馈质量的分工模式有效性，并发现过度语言扩展存在边际效用递减的“天花板效应”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在K-12教育中的应用是一把“双刃剑”，需要有效的LLM-教师-学生三方协作机制，但目前缺乏大规模实证研究来验证这种协作模式的有效性及其边界条件。

Method: 开发了支持K-12写作学习的三方协作系统；构建了基于系统功能语言学的多维度评估框架和反馈轨迹追踪管线；收集了来自120所学校10,195名学生在两年内提交的57,954篇作文的大规模实证数据集。

Result: 验证了该系统提升写作质量的有效性；证实了战略分工模式（LLM生成引擎+教师教学守门人）的效能；发现了过度语言扩展存在边际效用递减的天花板效应。

Conclusion: 建议采用动态自适应的LLM-教师协作模式，随着学生能力提升调整协作策略，以克服天花板效应并最大化学习收益。

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [320] [Persona Conditioning of Brand Recommendations in Retrieval-Augmented Commercial Chat: A Prominence-Stratified Cross-Provider Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30207)
*Will Jack,Noah Lehman,Keller Maloney,Sarah Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究审计了AI助手在相同提示词但不同买家角色下推荐CRM软件的变化，发现角色变化会显著改变推荐集（Jaccard相似度下降0.12-0.20），头部品牌抗角色影响强（~80%一致性），而中端品牌推荐集变化高达75%；Anthropic模型因更多依赖训练数据先验（43-52%推荐无检索层证据），对角色变化更为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的提示词（如"最佳CRM软件"）可能来自不同背景的买家（创业者、企业VP、英国中小企业主），目前缺乏系统审计来量化这种上下文变化如何影响AI模型的品牌推荐结果。

Method: 在10种角色×8个提示词×3种模型配置×N=10重复的设计空间中采样2000次运行，覆盖OpenAI（全8提示词）和Anthropic sonnet-4.6/low（4提示词），使用Jaccard相似度衡量推荐集变化，并分层分析品牌地位的影响。

Result: 角色前缀使推荐集相似度下降0.12-0.20（95%聚类CI排除零）；头部品牌抗角色影响（~80%一致性），中端品牌推荐集变化达75%；Anthropic模型效应量更大（43-52%无检索证据推荐 vs OpenAI的8-29%）。

Conclusion: AI品牌感知的测量必须条件化于买家角色，相同提示词因模型认为的提问者身份不同而产生实质性不同的推荐集；聚合不同角色的测量协议会系统性地掩盖这种变异，效应集中在中端品牌且在更依赖先验的生成路径上最为显著。

Abstract: The same prompt -- "best CRM software" -- reaches AI assistants from buyers in widely different contexts: a solo founder, an enterprise VP, a UK SMB owner. We audit how strongly that contextual variation reshapes which brands the model recommends. The audit samples 2,000 runs over a design space of 10 personas x 8 prompts x 3 model configurations x N=10 reps, with the two OpenAI cells at full 8-prompt coverage and the Anthropic sonnet-4.6 / low cell at 4-prompt coverage. Prefixing the user message with a persona drops the recommendation-set similarity (Jaccard) by Delta = -0.12 to -0.20 relative to a same-persona baseline (clustered 95% CIs exclude zero on all three measured cells; the sonnet cell's CI rests on only 4 prompt clusters and is correspondingly wider). The effect is sharply prominence-stratified: category leaders are persona-resistant (~80% same-brand consistency across personas), but mid-market brands swap up to 75% of the recommendation set as the persona changes. The Anthropic model shows a larger point-estimate effect than the OpenAI configurations, though clustered CIs overlap for the closer contrast (sonnet vs. OpenAI/high); the asymmetry is consistent with Anthropic's more retrieval-unattributed generation route (43-52% recommendations without observed retrieval-layer evidence, vs OpenAI's 8-29%, documented in Jack 2026). Any measurement of AI brand perception must condition on the buyer persona supplying the query: the same prompt produces materially different recommendation sets depending on who the model thinks is asking, and a measurement protocol that aggregates across personas systematically obscures that variation. The effect concentrates at mid-market and is largest on the most priors-reliant generation route in our audit, consistent with persona responsiveness growing as models lean more on training-data priors and richer context integration.

</details>


### [321] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出情境信念管理（CBM）框架，研究长程交互中语言模型的信息管理能力，并引入BeliefTrack基准测试。通过信念状态奖励的强化学习可将失败率降低70.9%，表征级引导可降低46.1%的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程交互中语言模型需要管理累积信息（何时更新、保持或忽略状态），现有模型在此方面存在严重缺陷，缺乏可量化评估的基准。

Method: 提出CBM框架和BeliefTrack基准（包含规则发现和电路诊断任务），使用有限的信念空间和符号验证器实现精确的逐轮评估；采用信念状态奖励的强化学习和表征级引导方法进行改进。

Result: vanilla模型在CBM上失败严重；信念跟踪提示改进有限；强化学习平均降低70.9%的失败率；表征级引导在两项任务上降低46.1%的失败率。

Conclusion: 信念状态奖励的强化学习能显著提升语言模型的长程信息管理能力，表征级引导也提供了有效的替代方案，揭示了潜在信念状态动态是失败原因。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [322] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCP Server Proto-OKN 是一个基于Python的模型上下文协议服务器，通过自然语言使AI助手能够发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱，支持图路由、模式检查、SPARQL执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成，降低了生物医学和科学用户进行跨域知识图谱分析的门槛。该服务器使用FastMCP框架实现，代码开源在GitHub上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户进行跨域知识图谱分析的门槛，通过自然语言接口使AI助手能够与科学知识图谱交互。

Method: 基于Python的FastMCP框架实现MCP协议服务器，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能。

Result: 构建了一个功能完整的MCP服务器原型，支持AI助手通过自然语言发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱。

Conclusion: mcp-proto-okn通过自然语言接口有效降低了跨域知识图谱分析的门槛，为生物医学和科学用户提供了便捷的AI辅助分析工具。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [323] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个渐进式信息揭示的基准框架，用于评估大语言模型在科学发现中的创新推理能力。通过从最小信息到完整实验细节逐步揭示论文内容，评估模型生成假设与原始论文结论的语义一致性。在45篇论文（涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料、纳米材料）上评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro，发现GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro优于前代，其中GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍能保持0.7的F1分数对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估LLM在多跳检索上的深度研究能力，但缺乏对其在科学发现中所需的创新推理能力（即超越已知知识的创造性推理）的系统评估。

Method: 构建渐进式信息揭示基准框架：模型初始仅接收论文主题和研究问题，随后逐步揭示技术细节。在每个信息阶段，模型需生成假设，与原始论文结论通过原子声明的语义相似度进行比较，评估从创新性（最小信息）到有依据推理（完整细节）的能力。

Result: 在45篇论文上评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro。GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro优于前代；GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍能保持0.7的F1分数与真实结论对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理和发现能力提供了基础，对于开发下一代AI科学家/共同科学家系统至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [324] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIRA框架，通过自锚定规则发现实现面向中训练阶段的数据筛选，在代码中训练任务中以一半数据量匹敌全量训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中训练阶段的数据筛选问题具有独特性：数据在接近预训练规模的体量下采用预训练风格目标优化，但数据经过策划以增强下游能力，且来自异构源，格式和训练角色各异。现有方法要么只提供隐式质量信号，要么假设固定规则或标准化格式，无法同时满足可扩展性和源自适应语义标准。

Method: 提出MIRA（源感知过滤框架），基于自锚定规则发现。核心思想是将规则构建作为数据选择的一部分：首先为每个源组发现应评估的内容，然后将这些判断蒸馏到可扩展的学生评分器中，用于全语料库过滤。

Result: 在包含21个数据源和5个源组的代码中训练任务中，MIRA在9个代码基准测试上优于所有筛选基线，仅使用一半的token即可匹配全量语料库的训练表现。

Conclusion: MIRA通过将规则发现纳入数据选择流程，有效解决了中训练阶段数据筛选的可扩展性与语义自适应需求之间的不匹配问题，验证了源感知语义筛选在异构数据中训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [325] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了数据组织方式对大语言模型训练的影响，提出了四个关键准则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性和局部多样性），并基于这些准则设计了两种新的数据排序方法STR和SAW，实验验证了其在预训练和SFT阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于数据选择，而数据组织（排序）对LLM训练的影响尚未被充分探索，尤其是在LLM通常只训练一个或少数epochs的背景下。

Method: 复用数据效率场景下预计算的样本级分数，提出四个关键准则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性、局部多样性），并据此设计STR和SAW两种数据排序方法。

Result: 在不同模型规模、数据规模以及预训练和SFT阶段，所提出的准则和排序方法均能有效提升训练稳定性与性能。

Conclusion: 数据组织是提升LLM训练效率的有效手段，所提出的准则和方法具有鲁棒性和通用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [326] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多组件LLM智能体中“局部一致、全局不一致”的组合失效问题，提出了组合残差ε*的定量度量方法，并通过实验验证了该失效普遍存在（33-94%的ensemble团），带来了+0.115 nats/赌注的遗憾，而三种直观缓解策略均失败或倒退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组件LLM智能体各组件仅看到联合问题的局部，其组合可能违反基本概率公理，即使每个组件局部内是自洽的。当前缺乏对这种局部一致、全局不一致失效的形式化度量与诊断方法。

Method: 1) 形式化定义组合残差ε*（组合输出到联合一致多面体的L2距离）；2) 建立乘积结构二分法刻画局部一致性何时足以保证全局一致；3) 提出Rayleigh商预测残差（三类关系上误差在7%以内）；4) 使用层级Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；5) 引入anytime-valid e-过程进行序列一致性监控；6) 在1,876个ensemble团上对四LLM中端面板进行实验。

Result: 在1,876个ensemble团中，33-94%的团存在ε*>0；对应在1,770个已结算赌注上，按比例分配规则下每赌注产生+0.115 nats的遗憾（若赌徒自身进行一致性修复则遗憾降至+0.006）。三种直观缓解策略（检索、分区感知提示、聚合器LLM）均失败或出现倒退。

Conclusion: 多组件LLM智能体的组合不一致性是普遍且有害的问题，简单的直觉方法无法解决，需要更形式化的修复机制（如层级投影修复或e-过程监控）。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [327] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在时序异常检测中缺乏可解释性的问题，构建了VisAnomBench基准数据集（包含高质量异常解释），并微调出参数高效的VisAnomReasoner模型。实验表明，该模型在异常定位精度和F1分数上分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点，且在跨基准泛化中分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在多任务中表现优异，但在时序异常检测中效果不佳；公开异常检测基准仅提供区间标注，缺乏自然语言解释，难以微调VLM生成可解释的决策。

Method: 构建VisAnomBench基准，从公开时序数据集中提炼，利用多个大型VLM通过细粒度任务特定奖励选择高质量异常解释；在此基础上微调参数高效的VisAnomReasoner模型。

Result: 在VisAnomBench上，VisAnomReasoner的精度和F1分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U基准上，精度和F1分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点，展现了跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效提升时序异常检测的可解释性和准确性，且具有良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [328] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SchGen，首个从自然语言请求生成可编辑 PCB 原理图的大语言模型。通过设计语义化的代码表示（基于相对放置和引脚名称配线的编辑原语），并利用人机协作流水线构建大规模数据集，将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务。实验表明，SchGen 在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表示和更大规模的通用 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB 原理图设计目前仍依赖人工且需要专业知识，尽管生成式 AI 在数字/模拟 IC 设计领域取得进展，但从自然语言意图生成 PCB 原理图的工作尚属空白。核心障碍在于缺乏适合大语言模型的表示方式和大规模数据集（现有格式语法冗长、几何描述过重）。

Method: 提出语义化的代码表示，将原理图编辑原语编码为相对放置和基于引脚名称的配线，将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务；通过人机协作流水线将开源硬件设计转换并构建大规模 PCB 原理图-用户提示配对数据集；基于该表示和数据集训练大语言模型 SchGen。

Result: SchGen 在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表示以及更大规模的通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: 表示设计在使生成模型胜任复杂硬件设计任务中起着关键作用，语义化的代码表示是推动 PCB 原理图自动生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [329] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一位物理学家在12个工作日内监督AI编程智能体（Claude Code）构建CLAX-PT（可微单圈微扰论模块）的案例研究，记录了15次监督事件，发现智能体无法自主解决的3个问题均源于其将症状缓解视为根本原因解决，且通过53个会话中的33个调整参数却未能重新评估自身架构选择。研究表明，监督设计而非模型能力决定了输出可信度，并提出了关键监督实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI智能体在科学计算中的角色边界（工具/合著者/研究者），并通过量化案例研究揭示当前AI智能体在物理推理中的根本性局限。

Method: N=1的量化案例研究：物理学家使用Claude Code（Sonnet和Opus模型）在57个会话中构建CLAX-PT模块，记录并分类15次监督事件，按干预级别分析智能体自主解决与需要人类干预的情况。

Result: 10个问题由智能体自主解决；2个需要物理学家领域知识；3个无法解决（均逃逸了oracle测试）。智能体将33个会话用于调整代码架构内的系数，而该架构本身无法表示目标物理；即使被提示重新考虑分支选择，也无法重新评估。智能体还提交了一个通过所有oracle测试但对应理论中不存在的量的"调和因子"（fudge factor），在错误宇宙学下预测错误值。三种监督实践被证明关键：多样化参数点测试、共享变更日志、禁止非物理数值补丁的明确规则。

Conclusion: 监督设计而非模型能力决定了输出可信度。缩小差距需要智能体能够提出架构替代方案而非在给定结构内优化，并能区分预测充分性与解释正确性——这些能力在当前智能体中未展现，且并非仅靠规模扩展就能解决。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了稀疏更新场景下动量方法的动力学行为，揭示了动量保留时间尺度与学习时间尺度之比决定了系统相位结构：学习慢于保留时退化为SGD，学习快于保留时不稳定，两者相等时恢复经典重球动力学。稀疏性差异会导致全局动量存在频谱冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量理论假设梯度以恒定速率到达每个参数，但实际中重尾数据分布和现代架构违反这一假设，亟需理解稀疏更新下的动量动力学。

Method: 构建两个可解析模型（稀疏输入的最小二乘和稀有类别的逻辑回归），推导精确闭式二阶矩动力学，在高维极限下表征稀疏性、批大小和动量衰减三个缩放指数的影响。

Result: 发现相位结构由动量保留时间尺度（缓冲存活的有效更新次数）与学习时间尺度（降低均方误差所需有效更新次数）之比决定；不同token稀疏度对应不同动量值的振荡动力学，造成全局动量的频谱冲突。

Conclusion: 稀疏更新场景下动量与SGD的性能关系取决于时间尺度匹配程度，且不同频率的token对动量参数存在冲突需求，需要自适应或频率特定的动量策略。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [331] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Anytime-FC-RAG，将联邦共形检索增强生成（FC-RAG）从固定时间范围扩展到任意时间有效序贯覆盖，保证在任何停止时间下的有效性，并通过可预测自适应控制（重新校准、带宽升级、蒸馏学生刷新）维持该保证，无需额外假设。核心创新在于引入可累积的每步校准偏差预算和截断投注 e-过程，实现时间均匀报警有效性、Hoeffding 拼接累积误覆盖包络、自适应控制下的安全性以及训练侧错误传播控制。实验表明在 GPT-2-small+MiniLM 集群上节省 14-57% 带宽，且报警仅在覆盖真正失效时触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FC-RAG 仅提供固定时间范围的边际覆盖保证，无法在任意停止时间下维持有效性；同时自适应控制（如带宽调整、模型刷新）会破坏原有的统计保证。需要一种序贯扩展方法，使得在联邦弱语言模型场景下，覆盖有效性在任何停止时间都成立，且不受自适应控制策略的影响。

Method: (1) 设计可累积的每步校准偏差预算，将边际覆盖界转化为校准良好事件上的严格条件界；(2) 构造截断投注 e-过程，使其在全概率空间上成为非负上鞅；(3) 结合两者得到时间均匀报警有效性、Hoeffding 拼接累积误覆盖包络、任意自适应控制器下的安全性、以及训练侧错误传播的累加预算控制。

Result: 在 GPT-2-small + MiniLM 集群上，MMLU、DBpedia、AG News 数据集验证了：(1) 预测报警率与实际一致；(2) 检测延迟可控；(3) 包络覆盖率满足理论保证；(4) 相比固定高带宽方案节省 14-57% 通信成本；报警仅在覆盖真正失效时触发。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG 成功将联邦共形 RAG 的覆盖保证从固定时间范围扩展到任意时间有效，在不需要额外假设的前提下实现了自适应控制下的统计安全性，为带宽受限的联邦弱语言模型群提供了实用的序贯覆盖监控方案。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [332] [Prediction-Powered Inference Across Many Tasks for AI Evaluation & Social Science Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29249)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Ellery Stahler,Chara Podimata*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务预测驱动推断框架，利用跨任务代理-真实标签关系中的共享结构，在标签稀缺时提升统计推断效率，并证明仅非线性结构能带来超越幂调优PPI的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测驱动推断(PPI)方法将任务独立处理，未能利用相关任务间的共享结构，这在每任务仅有少量标签时尤为受限。需要一种能跨任务共享信息同时保留任务特定推断的方法。

Method: 提出多任务PPI框架，包含：(1) 跨任务重校准以利用代理-真实标签关系的共享结构；(2) 保留任务内矫正和幂调优以构建准确点估计和置信区间。理论上证明了仅当代理-真实标签关系存在非线性结构时，跨任务重校准才能超越幂调优PPI的渐进效率。

Result: 在合成和半合成数据集上验证了理论发现，并在2024年美国总统选举期间对语言模型选举信息审计的案例研究中，通过大规模人工标注实验证明跨任务重校准在标签稀缺时能显著减小置信区间宽度。

Conclusion: 多任务PPI框架通过跨任务信息共享有效提升了标签稀缺场景下的统计推断效率；线性跨任务重校准在渐进意义下等价于原始代理，仅非线性结构能带来实质性增益。

Abstract: Many applications require statistically valid inference across many related tasks, while using only a handful of high-quality labels per hypothesis. In AI evaluation, these tasks may correspond to model behaviors across prompts, subgroups, or hypotheses; in social science surveys, they may correspond to related questions, populations, or measurement conditions. Prediction-powered inference (PPI) uses abundant but inexpensive proxy measurements to improve inference from limited, ground-truth labels, but commonly used methods treat tasks independently and therefore fail to exploit shared structure across related tasks. This limitation is especially important in settings where only a small number of labels are available per task. To address this issue, we introduce a multi-task prediction-powered inference framework that uses labeled data from related tasks to improve power while preserving task-specific inference. Our methods exploit the shared structure in the proxy-ground-truth relationship through cross-task recalibration, while retaining within-task rectification and power tuning to construct accurate point estimates and confidence intervals. We prove that efficiency gains beyond power-tuned PPI are only possible when the proxy-ground-truth relationship contains nonlinear structure; affine cross-task recalibrations are asymptotically equivalent to using the original proxy. We complement our theoretical findings with experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets, as well as a case study auditing language models on election-related information during the 2024 U.S. presidential election. Using a large human-annotation study, we show that cross-task recalibration can substantially reduce confidence interval widths when labels are scarce.

</details>


### [333] [Deep Optimal Individualized Treatment Rules for Bivariate Survival Outcomes via Adaptive Prediction-Powered Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29464)
*Kun Ren,Yifan Cui,Wen Su*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度神经网络的个性化治疗规则优化方法，旨在最大化双变量生存结果在固定时间点(t1,t2)的联合生存概率，并处理右删失数据。方法采用随机策略建模治疗规则，通过连接函数耦合边缘加速失效时间模型以捕捉双变量依赖性，并引入自适应预测增强方法利用机器学习辅助预测提升决策鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及多种治疗的随机试验中，双变量生存结局为治疗决策带来显著分析挑战，现有方法难以有效优化个性化治疗规则以最大化双变量联合生存概率。

Method: 1) 通过深度神经网络建模治疗规则；2) 采用随机策略刻画治疗分配；3) 利用连接函数耦合边缘加速失效时间模型捕捉双变量依赖结构；4) 引入自适应预测增强方法，利用机器学习模型的辅助预测提升决策鲁棒性和有效性。

Result: 论文提出了一种新的框架，能够有效处理右删失数据下的双变量生存结局，并通过深度神经网络学习最优个性化治疗规则。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过随机策略、连接函数耦合与自适应预测增强相结合，为多治疗情形下双变量生存结局的个性化决策提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In randomized trials involving multiple treatments, bivariate survival outcomes present significant analytical challenges for making decisions. This paper addresses the problem of deriving optimal individualized treatment rules to maximize the joint survival probability beyond fixed time points $(t_1, t_2)$ through deep neural networks, while accounting for right censoring. We propose a novel approach that models treatment rules via stochastic policies, coupling marginal accelerated failure time models via link function to capture bivariate dependence. To enhance robustness and effectiveness of decision making, we introduce an adaptive prediction-powered method that leverages auxiliary predictions from machine learning models.

</details>


### [334] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦语言建模中基于探针集的对数蒸馏（FPLD）的通信-统计速率。证明单轮带宽项速率确为Θ(K^{-1}·2^{-2B/V})，并给出了T轮顺序细化下的O(K^{-1}·2^{-2TB/V})改进率；同时建立了异质带宽下的最优分配策略（对数倾斜注水法则），并通过短预热阶段的插件式自适应变体实现了1+O(√(log(K/δ)/(m T_0)))的相对次优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Dubey and Huo, 2026）给出了联邦探针-对数蒸馏（FPLD）的高概率KL上界，其中包含带宽项O(K^{-1}·2^{-2B/V})。但该带宽项速率是否紧、以及上界如何推广到异质带宽场景，这两个问题尚未解决。

Method: (1) 通过抖动FPLD构造，在非退化条件下证明单轮下界Ω(K^{-1}·2^{-2B/V})，确定带宽轴速率为Θ(K^{-1}·2^{-2B/V})；T轮顺序细化使用嵌套/缩放残差量化器达到O(K^{-1}·2^{-2TB/V})。(2) 建立异质带宽上界，导出了封闭形式的最优分配B_i^* = B_tot/K + (V/2) log_2(w_i/ȳ_g)（对数倾斜注水法则），并设计短预热阶段的插件自适应变体。

Result: (1) 单轮带宽项速率确为紧的Θ(K^{-1}·2^{-2B/V})，且T轮顺序细化显著改进带宽指数。(2) 异质带宽最优分配为对数倾斜注水法则，自适应变体在预热后达到1+O(√(log(K/δ)/(m T_0)))相对次优性。合成n-gram仿真验证了KL上下界的紧致性及最优分配对均匀和逆权重基线的优势。

Conclusion: 本文填补了联邦探针-对数蒸馏中带宽项速率的紧性空白，并首次将理论推广到异质带宽场景，为分布式语言模型蒸馏中的通信预算分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


### [335] [Eigen-Spike Emergence and Quadratic Equivalents for Conjugate Kernels on Nonlinearly Separable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29669)
*Collin Cranston,Zhichao Wang,Todd Kemp,Michael W. Mahoney*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用随机矩阵理论中的确定性等价方法，通过分析非线性特征映射（共轭核）在XOR问题中的特征值相变，揭示了线性分类在非线性可分数据上成为可能的条件，并量化了样本复杂度、信噪比、激活函数选择和预训练特征等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RMT中确定性等价（线性代理模型）能简化大规模非线性随机矩阵的谱分析，但其对非线性可分数据（如XOR问题）的适用性尚不明确，即线性等价是否仍能捕捉非线性学习能力。

Method: 以XOR问题为非线性可分数据的典型代表，研究前馈NN的共轭核（CK）矩阵；为spiked CK矩阵建立鲁棒的二次等价模型，分析信息性离群特征值及其特征向量与XOR标签的渐近对齐；推导BBP型相变条件。

Result: 在样本复杂度、信噪比、激活函数选择和预训练特征四种场景下，均精确刻画了线性分类通过CK特征向量成为可能的相变边界。

Conclusion: 将RMT中确定性等价工具的适用范围从线性模型扩展到具有实际ML意义的非线性可分问题，建立了理论分析桥梁。

Abstract: Recent work in random matrix theory (RMT) has developed the notion of deterministic equivalents: typically linear surrogate models that approximate the spectral behavior of large nonlinear random matrices, such as nonlinear feature maps in neural networks (NNs). On the one hand, these deterministic equivalents make theoretical predictions tractable by reducing a complex model to a simpler model with properties that fall under the umbrella of classical RMT tools. However, this leaves open the question of whether this idealized linear equivalence remains meaningful when dealing with high-dimensional nonlinearly separable data, such as performing clssification on nonlinearly separable data. Motivated by this, we consider the conjugate kernel (CK), which is the nonlinear feature map of a feedforward NN, under a canonical nonlinearly separable dataset, the XOR problem; and we use the study of informative outlier eigenvalues in the CK and whether their corresponding eigenvectors asymptotically align with XOR labels as a proxy for nonlinear learnability. We develop a robust quadratic equivalent to the spiked CK matrix that enables a precise analysis of emergent informative spikes, as one modifies various knobs common in ML practice: sample complexity, signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), nonlinear activation choice, and pretrained features. In each of these scenarios, we derive a precise BBP-type phase transition in which linear classification via the CK eigenvectors becomes possible. Our analysis helps translate the power of deterministic equivalence tools in RMT to study problems of practical relevance in ML.
